Original Abstract: Incremental object detection (IOD) aims to extend detectors to new categories while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Existing methods often adopt a class incremental learning perspective, separating feature spaces to sharpen decision boundaries. However, this separation-oriented paradigm may overlook object symbiosis in detection, where co-occurrence and occlusion introduce spatial and semantic dependencies that benefit from shared representations. Ignoring these dependencies distorts the shared representations, exacerbates confusion between old and new classes, and accelerates catastrophic forgetting. To address this, we propose Symbiosis-Inspired Knowledge Distillation (SIKD), which explicitly leverages object symbiosis at two complementary levels. Spatial Symbiosis Distillation (SpSD) focuses on symbiotic regions where the old model responds with high overlap to objects in the new task. It preserves generalizable old class cues, suppresses class-specific bias and redundancy, and distills the refined evidence to the new model at matched spatial locations with slot-aligned supervision. Semantic Symbiosis Distillation (SeSD) maintains class level structure by forming confidence weighted prototypes for old classes and aligning their inter class soft ranks over the old class logits, which stabilizes the semantic topology during adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of the proposed method.

中文摘要翻译: 增量目标检测（IOD）旨在将检测器扩展到新类别，同时保留先前获得的知识。现有方法通常采用类增量学习视角，通过分离特征空间来锐化决策边界。然而，这种面向分离的范式可能忽视了检测中的目标共生关系——共现和遮挡引入了空间和语义依赖，而这些依赖本可以从共享表征中获益。忽视这些依赖会扭曲共享表征，加剧新旧类之间的混淆，并加速灾难性遗忘。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了共生启发的知识蒸馏（SIKD），在两个互补层面显式利用目标共生关系。空间共生蒸馏（SpSD）聚焦于旧模型对新任务目标响应具有高重叠的共生区域，保留可泛化的旧类线索，抑制类别特定的偏见和冗余，并通过插槽对齐监督在匹配的空间位置上将精炼后的证据蒸馏给新模型。语义共生蒸馏（SeSD）通过为旧类构建置信度加权的原型，并在旧类logits上对齐它们的类间软排序来维持类别级结构，从而在适应过程中稳定语义拓扑。大量实验证明了所提方法的有效性和优越性。

</details>


### [30] [GeoAnchor: Collaborative Reasoning via Latent Decomposition for 3D Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13454)
*Hao Li,Han Fang,Zixin Pan,Xin Wei,Hongbo Sun,Jinglin Xu,Zhiyu Lin,Ye Yuan,Zhongjiang He,Yu Yu,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoAnchor提出了一种交错文本-潜变量推理框架，通过将3D空间信息分解为位置、方向和几何三种互补的潜变量组件，并配合协作训练策略，在多样化的复杂3D推理任务上取得了最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLM在从2D图像理解3D空间关系时存在两个关键局限：1）依赖符号文本token，无法保真地表示连续几何信息；2）单一潜变量类型无法适应多样化空间任务，导致复杂几何场景中的对齐错误。

Method: GeoAnchor框架包含三个核心设计：1）将3D空间信息分解为位置潜变量（目标定位）、方向潜变量（关系朝向）和几何潜变量（场景结构）；2）在结构化空间中重组这些组件以构建局部证据并捕获全局上下文；3）引入协作训练策略，引导模型从局部空间感知逐步过渡到全面的3D理解。

Result: 在多样化和复杂的3D推理任务上，GeoAnchor优于现有最先进方法，验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: GeoAnchor通过多类型潜变量分解与重组的方式，有效解决了从2D图像理解3D空间关系的挑战，实现了动态且可解释的推理，并具有良好的泛化性能。

Original Abstract: Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress, understanding 3D spatial relationships from 2D images remains a critical challenge. Existing methods primarily rely on symbolic text tokens, which inherently lack the fidelity to represent continuous geometric information. While recent methods use latent representations to enhance reasoning, relying on a single latent type cannot adapt to the diversity of spatial tasks, leading to misalignment in complex geometric scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose GeoAnchor, an interleaved text-latent reasoning framework. GeoAnchor decomposes 3D spatial information into three complementary components: position latents for object grounding, direction latents for relational orientation, and geometry latents for scene structure. These components are recombined in a structured space to construct local evidence while capturing global context, enabling dynamic and interpretable reasoning. Furthermore, we introduce a collaborative training strategy that guides the model from local spatial perception to comprehensive 3D understanding. Extensive experiments on diverse and complex 3D reasoning tasks demonstrate that GeoAnchor outperforms the state of the art, validating its effectiveness and generalization capabilities.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）取得了显著进展，但从二维图像理解三维空间关系仍然是一个关键挑战。现有方法主要依赖符号文本标记，这些标记本质上缺乏表示连续几何信息的保真度。虽然近期方法使用潜变量表示来增强推理能力，但依赖单一潜变量类型无法适应空间任务的多样性，导致在复杂几何场景中出现对齐错误。为应对这些局限性，我们提出了GeoAnchor，一种交错文本-潜变量推理框架。GeoAnchor将三维空间信息分解为三个互补组件：用于目标定位的位置潜变量、用于关系朝向的方向潜变量和用于场景结构的几何潜变量。这些组件在结构化空间中被重新组合，以构建局部证据同时捕获全局上下文，从而实现动态且可解释的推理。此外，我们引入了一种协作训练策略，引导模型从局部空间感知逐步迈向全面的三维理解。在多样化和复杂的三维推理任务上的大量实验表明，GeoAnchor优于现有最先进方法，验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

</details>


### [31] [TreeSRNF: Square-Root Normal Fields for Generative Modelling of the Geometric and Structural Variability in Tree-like 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13456)
*Tahmina Khanam,Hamid Laga,Mohammed Bennamoun,Guanjin Wang,Ferdous Sohel,Farid Boussaid,Anuj Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于分析和生成复杂树状3D物体（如植物树木）的数学框架，将树状物体建模为黎曼树形空间中的点，通过新型度量衡量表面弯曲、拉伸和结构变化，实现了树状3D物体的统计分析和合成，性能显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工作要么只考虑树状物体的骨架结构，要么仅用分支厚度近似其3D几何，缺乏同时精确建模分支3D几何及其连接方式的方法。

Method: 首先将原本用于属-0曲面统计分析的平方根法场（SRNF）表示推广到树状3D物体；然后将树状3D物体视为新型黎曼树形空间中的点，该空间配备的新黎曼度量可度量将一个树状3D物体对齐到另一个所需的面弯曲、拉伸和结构变化量；最后开发了计算逐点和逐分支对应关系以及复杂3D树之间测地路径的算法。

Result: 在真实和合成的植物及树木数据集上，所提框架显著优于现有最先进方法，能够有效计算统计摘要（均值和变异模式）并通过从概率分布采样合成新颖的树状3D物体。

Conclusion: 该框架为树状3D物体的几何与结构联合分析提供了理论严谨且实用的解决方案，在计算机图形学、植物建模和相关领域具有广泛应用前景。

Original Abstract: We introduce a novel mathematical framework for analyzing and generating complex tree-shaped 3D objects, such as botanical trees and plants, which deform both in their 3D geometry and branching structure. Unlike previous works, which either consider only the skeletal structure of tree-like objects or approximate their 3D geometry using branch thickness, the proposed framework accurately models both the 3D geometry of the tree branches and the way they are interconnected. In this paper, we first generalize the Square Root Normal Fields (SRNF) representation, originally proposed for the statistical analysis of genus-0 surfaces, to tree-shaped 3D objects. We then treat tree-shaped 3D objects as points on a novel Riemannian tree-shape space equipped with a novel Riemannian metric that measures the amount of surface bending and stretching, and structural changes one needs to apply to one 3D tree-shape to align it with another. This way, deformations become trajectories in this novel tree-shape space. We analyze the theoretical properties of this novel tree-shape space and the corresponding metric and develop algorithms for computing point-wise and branch-wise correspondences and geodesic paths between complex 3D trees. We finally show how to use these building blocks for (1) computing statistical summaries, \ie means and modes of variation, of collections of tree-shaped 3D objects, and (2) synthesizing novel tree-shaped 3D objects by sampling from probability distributions fitted to a population of tree-shaped 3D objects. We demonstrate the performance and utility of the proposed framework on real and synthetic plants and botanical trees and show that it significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一个新的数学框架，用于分析和生成复杂的树状3D物体（如植物和树木），这些物体在三维几何和分支结构上均存在形变。与以往仅考虑树状物体骨架结构或使用分支厚度近似其3D几何的工作不同，所提出的框架精确建模了树分支的3D几何及其互连方式。在本文中，我们首先将原本为属-0曲面统计分析提出的平方根法场（SRNF）表示推广到树状3D物体。然后，我们将树状3D物体视为一个新型黎曼树形空间中的点，该空间配备了一种新的黎曼度量，该度量衡量将一个3D树形对齐到另一个所需的表面弯曲、拉伸以及结构变化量。这样，形变便成为这个新型树形空间中的轨迹。我们分析了这一新型树形空间及其对应度量的理论性质，并开发了计算复杂3D树之间逐点和逐分支对应关系及测地路径的算法。最后，我们展示了如何利用这些基础构建块来（1）计算树状3D物体集合的统计摘要（即均值和变异模式），以及（2）通过从拟合到树状3D物体总体的概率分布中采样来合成新颖的树状3D物体。我们在真实和合成的植物及树木上展示了所提框架的性能和实用性，并证明其显著优于现有最先进方法。

</details>


### [32] [2D Rotary Position Embedding for Scene Text Recognition with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13458)
*Zobeir Raisi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出2D-RoPE-STR，通过自适应各向异性轴分配和将旋转位置编码扩展到编码器-解码器交叉注意力，使得场景文本识别模型能够利用二维空间布局信息，在不增加参数和架构改动的情况下，显著提升对弯曲、旋转和透视变形等不规则文本的识别性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的STR方法通常使用一维位置编码，忽略了文本图像的二维空间结构；而现有的2D RoPE扩展主要面向视觉Transformer，假设图像为各向同性的方形内容，且仅在编码器自注意力中应用旋转编码。场景文本图像高度各向异性（长宽比明显），且STR模型采用编码器-解码器结构，解码器需通过交叉注意力关联查询与编码器的二维布局，此前无相关工作解决这一设定。

Method: (1) 提出各向异性行列维度分配策略，根据文本图像的长宽比自适应分配行/列旋转编码维度； (2) 将轴向2D-RoPE扩展到编码器-解码器交叉注意力中，使自回归解码步骤能够根据编码器token的二维布局进行注意力计算。两项改进本质上是无参数的，仅需修改位置编码模块，无需重新设计架构。

Result: 在IIIT5K、SVT、ICDAR 2013、ICDAR 2015、CUTE80、SVTP六个标准基准上，性能提升集中在弯曲、旋转和透视变形等不规则文本布局上。通过消融实验（仅替换位置编码的控制对比、图像级净胜分歧分析、编码器注意力可视化）验证了相对二维位置在偏离水平直线的文本布局中发挥关键作用。

Conclusion: 2D-RoPE-STR通过无参数的位置编码改进，有效提升了STR在不规则文本上的识别能力，证明了在编码器-解码器架构中引入二维相对位置信息的重要性，为STR领域提供了一种轻量高效的位置编码方案。

Original Abstract: Scene Text Recognition (STR) remains challenging due to the diversity of text appearances, including curvature, rotation, and perspective distortion. Recent Transformer-based approaches perform well but usually rely on one-dimensional positional encodings that ignore the 2D spatial structure of text images. Axial 2D extensions of Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) exist for vision Transformers, but they assume roughly square, isotropic image content and apply the rotation only within encoder self-attention. Scene text violates both assumptions: crops are markedly anisotropic, and STR models are encoder-decoder, so the decoder must relate its queries to the encoder's 2D layout through cross-attention. We introduce 2D-RoPE-STR, which adapts axial 2D-RoPE to this setting through (1) an anisotropic row/column dimension allocation matched to the aspect ratio of text, and (2) an extension of the rotary coupling into encoder-decoder cross-attention, letting autoregressive decoding steps attend to encoder tokens by their 2D layout, a setting not addressed by prior encoder-only formulations. Both changes are essentially parameter-free and require no architectural redesign beyond the positional-encoding module. We further introduce a diagnostic protocol (a controlled ablation pair isolating only the positional encoding, an image-level net-win disagreement analysis, and encoder attention visualization) that identifies where and why relative 2D position helps: curved, rotated, and perspective-distorted layouts where reading order departs from a straight horizontal line. On six standard benchmarks (IIIT5K, SVT, ICDAR 2013, ICDAR 2015, CUTE80, SVTP), gains concentrate on exactly these irregular layouts, with ablations isolating each design choice against 1D RoPE and 2D sinusoidal and learnable alternatives.

中文摘要翻译: 场景文本识别（STR）由于文本外观的多样性（包括弯曲、旋转和透视变形）仍然具有挑战性。近年来基于Transformer的方法表现良好，但通常依赖于一维位置编码，忽略了文本图像的二维空间结构。针对视觉Transformer的轴向2D旋转位置编码（RoPE）扩展已被提出，但它们假设图像内容大致为方形的各向同性分布，且仅在编码器自注意力中应用旋转编码。场景文本违反了这两个假设：裁剪图像具有明显的各向异性，且STR模型采用编码器-解码器结构，解码器必须通过交叉注意力将其查询与编码器的二维布局关联起来。我们提出2D-RoPE-STR，通过以下方式将轴向2D-RoPE适配到该设定中：(1) 根据文本的长宽比进行各向异性的行/列维度分配；(2) 将旋转耦合扩展到编码器-解码器交叉注意力中，使自回归解码步骤能够根据编码器token的二维布局进行注意力计算——这是此前仅编码器的公式化方法未涉及的设定。这两项改进本质上是无参数的，且除位置编码模块外无需重新设计架构。我们还引入了一套诊断协议（仅隔离位置编码的控制消融对、图像级净胜分歧分析以及编码器注意力可视化），用以识别相对二维位置在何处以及为何能够发挥作用：在弯曲、旋转和透视变形的布局中，阅读顺序偏离了水平直线。在六个标准基准（IIIT5K、SVT、ICDAR 2013、ICDAR 2015、CUTE80、SVTP）上，性能提升恰好集中在这些不规则布局上，消融实验将每种设计选择与一维RoPE、二维正弦和可学习位置编码进行了隔离对比。

</details>


### [33] [LPM: Industrial-Scale Generative Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13460)
*Bichuan Zhu,Fulin Li,Jiachao Gong,Jinhua Hao,Kai Zhao,Kun Yuan,Pengcheng Xu,Qiang Wang,Qiao Mo,Yanlong Yuan,Yizhen Shao,Yuxiao Hu,Zixi Tuo,Ming Sun,Chao Zhou,Bin Chen,Bin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LPM是一个基于扩散模型的生成式视频恢复框架，已工业级部署于快手，覆盖约45%观看时长，在提升画质的同时实现20%码率节省，年带宽成本节省数亿。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 用户生成内容（UGC）中存在复杂多样的退化问题（如模糊、噪声、压缩伪影等），现有视频恢复方法在真实世界退化场景下难以兼顾高保真度、时间一致性和大规模部署的实用性。

Method: 提出LPM框架，包含三个核心组件：1）大规模数据工程构建高质量训练数据；2）增强架构+渐进式训练策略训练基础模型；3）时间金字塔推理机制支持任意时长视频的高效、时域一致恢复。

Result: 在快手生产环境部署后，处理视频占平台总观看时长约45%；在同等感知质量下相比快手内部编解码器降低20%码率，年带宽成本节省数亿；低服务成本使其可集成至Kling等产品。

Conclusion: 生成式视频恢复在工业级大规模部署中是可行、可扩展且成本有效的，能够同时带来画质提升和系统级收益（如带宽节省）。

Original Abstract: We present the Large Processing Model (LPM), a diffusion-based generative framework for photorealistic video restoration under complex, in-the-wild degradations. To our knowledge, LPM is the first generative video restoration model deployed at industrial scale. LPM addresses the diverse degradations in user-generated content (UGC) through a unified system encompassing large-scale data engineering, foundation-model training, and efficient inference. Its enhanced architecture, progressive training strategy, and temporal-pyramid inference mechanism jointly enable high-fidelity, temporally consistent restoration of arbitrarily long videos across the broad content distribution encountered on UGC platforms. LPM has been deployed in production at Kuaishou, where videos processed by the model account for approximately 45% of total viewing time, delivering consistent improvements across key quality-of-experience metrics. Beyond perceptual enhancement, LPM delivers substantial system-level benefits: at comparable perceptual quality, it reduces bitrate by 20% relative to Kuaishou's in-house codec, yielding annual bandwidth cost savings on the order of hundreds of millions. Its low serving cost also enables integration into products such as Kling, demonstrating that generative restoration can be practical, scalable, and cost-effective for large-scale video processing.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了大规模处理模型（LPM），一种基于扩散的生成式框架，用于在复杂、真实世界退化条件下实现逼真的视频恢复。据我们所知，LPM是首个被部署于工业规模的生成式视频恢复模型。LPM通过一个统一的系统应对用户生成内容（UGC）中的多样化退化问题，该系统涵盖了大规模数据工程、基础模型训练和高效推理。其增强的架构、渐进式训练策略和时间金字塔推理机制共同实现了对UGC平台上广泛内容分布中的任意长度视频的高保真、时域一致的恢复。LPM已在快手生产环境中部署，经过该模型处理的视频占总观看时长的约45%，在关键体验质量指标上持续带来改善。除感知增强外，LPM还带来显著的系统级收益：在可比感知质量下，相比快手内部编解码器可降低20%的码率，每年节省数亿级别的带宽成本。其低服务成本还使其能够集成到Kling等产品中，证明了生成式恢复对于大规模视频处理是实用、可扩展且经济高效的。

</details>


### [34] [HIVE-3D: Hierarchical Voxel Enhancement for High-Quality 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13468)
*Bin Zang,Wenting Zheng,Xiaoliang Luo,Zhiyuan Fang,Shi Li,Lvchun Wang,Wei Yu,Yi Zhao,Tian Xie,Yuchi Huo,Rengan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HIVE-3D，一种基于分层体素增强框架的从单张图像生成高质量3D场景的方法，通过图像分割、注意力检索、层次化组件树和体素超分辨率模型，实现从粗到细的3D场景重建，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于单张图像的3D生成方法受限于表示分辨率不足，无法适用于3D场景的高质量生成。

Method: 1) 输入单张场景图像，先生成粗糙初始场景；2) 引入图像分割和基于注意力机制的检索，对齐2D图像组件与3D场景组件；3) 将场景关系组织为层次化组件树（叶子节点表示更细粒度的组件）；4) 提出体素超分辨率模型，在保持与粗体素强一致性的同时生成目标实例的精细体素；5) 对每个组件的图像和体素执行从粗到细的层次化超分辨率，最终生成高分辨率高质量3D场景。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法显著优于之前的方法，达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: HIVE-3D通过分层体素增强框架有效解决了单张图像3D场景生成中的分辨率限制问题，实现了高质量3D场景生成。

Original Abstract: Recently, a line of works can generate impressive 3D objects from a single image, but they are limited by restricted representation resolution, making them unsuitable for 3D scene generation. In this work, we introduce HIVE-3D, a novel method for high-quality 3D scene generation based on hierarchical voxel enhancement framework. Specifically, given a single scene image as input, we first produce a coarse initial scene, then introduce image segmentation and attention-based retrieval to align 2D image components with 3D scene components. Subsequently, we organize these scene relations into a hierarchical component tree, where nodes closer to the leaves denote finer-grained components. Finally, we propose a voxel super-resolution model that generates refined voxels for the target instance while maintaining strong consistency with the coarse voxels. Equipped with this model, we perform coarse-to-fine hierarchical super-resolution on images and voxels for each component, producing a high-resolution and high-quality 3D scene. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms previous approaches, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

中文摘要翻译: 近期，一系列工作能够从单张图像生成令人印象深刻的3D物体，但受限于有限的表示分辨率，无法适用于3D场景生成。本文提出HIVE-3D，一种基于分层体素增强框架的高质量3D场景生成方法。具体来说，给定单张场景图像作为输入，我们首先生成一个粗糙的初始场景，然后引入图像分割和基于注意力机制的检索，将2D图像组件与3D场景组件对齐。随后，我们将这些场景关系组织成一个层次化组件树，其中靠近叶子节点的节点表示更细粒度的组件。最后，我们提出一个体素超分辨率模型，该模型在保持与粗体素强一致性的同时，为目标实例生成精细体素。借助该模型，我们对每个组件的图像和体素执行从粗到细的层次化超分辨率，从而生成高分辨率高质量的3D场景。大量实验表明，我们的方法显著优于之前的方法，达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [35] [GPOcc++: Unified Sparse Gaussian Occupancy Prediction with Visual Geometry Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13481)
*Changqing Zhou,Yueru Luo,Yulan Guo,Bing Wang,Jie Qin,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GPOcc/GPOcc++ 提出将视觉几何先验转换为 occupancy-aware 稀疏高斯表示，实现高效且表达力强的三维场景体素占用预测，在室内外多视图和时序设定下均表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉几何先验本质上是表面中心的，而占用预测需要推理体素内部和自由空间，两者存在表征鸿沟。

Method: 提出 GPOcc，将视觉几何先验转换为 occupancy-aware 稀疏高斯表示进行体素场景建模；在此基础上 GPOcc++ 将多视图观测和时序序列统一在同一框架下，并推广至室外场景。

Result: 在室内外多个基准上，多视图和时序设定下均取得一致强性能，同时具备良好的效率和泛化性。

Conclusion: 所提的稀疏高斯表示能有效桥接表面几何先验与体素占用预测之间的鸿沟，且框架统一、高效、泛化能力强。

Original Abstract: Accurate 3D scene understanding is fundamental to embodied intelligence and autonomous driving, where 3D occupancy provides a unified representation of objects, structures, and free space. However, recovering such a complete volumetric representation from visual observations remains challenging, particularly in occluded and unobserved regions. Visual geometry priors offer strong and generalizable geometric cues for addressing this challenge, but their outputs are inherently surface-centric, whereas occupancy prediction requires reasoning about volumetric interiors and free space. To bridge this gap, we introduce GPOcc, which transforms visual geometry priors into occupancy-aware sparse Gaussian representations for efficient and expressive volumetric scene modeling. Building on GPOcc, GPOcc++ models multi-view observations and temporal sequences within a unified framework, allowing spatial and temporal evidence to be handled through the same representation. We further extend GPOcc++ from indoor scenes to outdoor occupancy prediction. Extensive experiments on both indoor and outdoor benchmarks demonstrate consistently strong performance across both multi-view and temporal settings, together with favorable efficiency and generalization. Code will be released at https://github.com/JuIvyy/GPOcc.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的三维场景理解是具身智能和自动驾驶的基础，其中三维占用提供了一种统一表示物体、结构和自由空间的方式。然而，从视觉观测中恢复完整的体素表示仍然具有挑战性，尤其是在遮挡和未观测区域。视觉几何先验为解决该问题提供了强且可泛化的几何线索，但其输出本质上是表面中心的，而占用预测需要推理体素内部和自由空间。为弥合这一鸿沟，我们引入了 GPOcc，将视觉几何先验转化为 occupancy-aware 稀疏高斯表示，用于高效且富有表现力的体素场景建模。在 GPOcc 基础上，GPOcc++ 将多视图观测和时序序列建模在统一框架中，使得空间和时间证据能够通过相同的表示进行处理。我们进一步将 GPOcc++ 从室内场景扩展到室外占用预测。在室内和室外基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在多视图和时序设定下均取得了一致优异的性能，同时兼具良好的效率和泛化性。代码将发布于 https://github.com/JuIvyy/GPOcc。

</details>


### [36] [CASA-SDF: Curriculum-Aware Spatial Adaptation with Curvature-Guided Density for Neural Implicit Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13492)
*Lei Yang,Weiqing Li,Zhiyong Su,Liang Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CASA-SDF提出了一种统一框架，通过空间自适应监督和表示能力互补适配，解决室内场景中大面积纹理平坦区域与薄结构之间的几何异构性问题，实现了高保真表面重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 室内场景存在显著的几何异构性：大面积无纹理平面需要强正则化抑制高频伪影，而薄结构则需要更锐利的表示以避免MLP谱偏导致的过平滑。现有方法依赖空间无差别的先验监督和全局SDF-密度变换，难以平衡平面平滑与细节保真。

Method: 1) 混合空间自适应不确定性退火（SAUA）：融合语义和光度不确定性构建像素级课程学习策略，用于单目先验监督，在可靠区域维持正则化，早期削弱不可靠监督以允许数据驱动的光度精细化。2) 曲率感知局部自适应密度变换（CALADT）：通过曲率代理渐进调制SDF-密度映射的锐度，增强薄结构表示。

Result: 在室内基准数据集上的大量实验表明，CASA-SDF在保持平面稳定性的同时，提升了高频结构的表面完整性和细节恢复能力。

Conclusion: CASA-SDF通过课程感知的空间自适应监督和局部自适应密度变换，有效解决了室内场景几何异构性问题，在不牺牲平面区域稳定性的前提下显著改善了薄结构的重建质量。

Original Abstract: Neural implicit representations have emerged as a powerful paradigm for 3D reconstruction. However, high-fidelity indoor surface reconstruction remains a significant challenge, primarily due to the pronounced \emph{geometric heterogeneity} of indoor scenes. Large texture-less planar regions typically require stronger regularization to suppress high-frequency artifacts, while thin structures demand sharper, more adaptive representations to mitigate the spectral bias of multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) and prevent over-smoothing. Existing approaches often rely on spatially indiscriminate prior supervision and a scene-global SDF-to-density transformation, which constrains their ability to balance planar smoothness and detail preservation. In this paper, we propose CASA-SDF (Curriculum-Aware Spatial Adaptation for SDF), a unified framework that addresses this challenge via complementary adaptations of supervision and representation capacity. Specifically, Hybrid Spatially-Adaptive Uncertainty Annealing (SAUA) fuses semantic and photometric uncertainties to construct a pixel-wise curriculum for monocular prior supervision. This strategy maintains regularization in reliable regions while attenuating unreliable supervision early in training to enable data-driven photometric refinement. Meanwhile, Curvature-Aware Locally Adaptive Density Transformation (CALADT) progressively modulates the sharpness of the SDF-to-density mapping via a curvature proxy to enhance the representation of thin structures. Extensive experiments on benchmark indoor datasets demonstrate that CASA-SDF improves surface completeness and detail recovery on high-frequency structures, without compromising the stability of planar surfaces.

中文摘要翻译: 神经隐式表示已成为3D重建的强大范式。然而，高保真室内表面重建仍然是一个重大挑战，主要原因是室内场景存在显著的几何异构性。大面积无纹理平面区域通常需要更强的正则化来抑制高频伪影，而薄结构则需要更锐利、更具适应性的表示来缓解多层感知器（MLP）的谱偏问题并防止过平滑。现有方法通常依赖空间无差别的先验监督和场景全局的SDF-密度变换，这限制了它们在平衡平面平滑与细节保持方面的能力。本文提出CASA-SDF（即课程感知的空间自适应SDF），一个通过监督能力和表示能力的互补适配来解决该挑战的统一框架。具体而言，混合空间自适应不确定性退火（SAUA）融合语义不确定性和光度不确定性，构建像素级的课程学习策略用于单目先验监督。该策略在可靠区域维持正则化，同时在训练早期减弱不可靠监督，以便进行数据驱动的光度精细化。同时，曲率感知局部自适应密度变换（CALADT）通过曲率代理渐进调制SDF-密度映射的锐度，增强薄结构的表示能力。在室内基准数据集上的大量实验表明，CASA-SDF在不牺牲平面区域稳定性的前提下，提升了高频结构的表面完整性和细节恢复能力。

</details>


### [37] [M2P-AD: Memory-to-Prototype Learning with Boundary-aware Score Refinement for 3D Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13499)
*Seyoung Jeong,Jong Pil Yun,Sang Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为M2P-AD的3D异常检测模型，通过记忆到原型模块和边界感知评分修正，有效抑制正常区域的过高异常响应和物体边界附近的误报，在多个基准数据集上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D异常检测方法在正常区域会产生过高的异常响应，且在物体边界附近存在大量误报，这些问题尚未得到有效解决。

Method: 提出M2P-AD模型，包含三个关键组件：(1) Memory-to-Prototype (M2P)模块，从正常特征嵌入中学习代表性原型以保留物体结构信息；(2) 边界提取 (BE)模块，用于识别物体边界；(3) 边界感知评分修正 (BSR)策略，结合边界特征重新校准异常分数。

Result: 在Real3D-AD、Anomaly-ShapeNet和MulSen-AD三个基准数据集上取得了最先进的性能。定性结果表明，正常区域的过高异常分数被降低，物体边界附近的误报被有效抑制，异常定位更准确稳定。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够实现更可靠的3D异常检测，为实际工业环境提供了鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: 3D anomaly detection has recently emerged as an important research topic in computer vision. Although existing methods have achieved high performance, excessive anomaly responses in normal regions and false positives near object boundaries remain unresolved challenges. To address these challenges, we propose a novel 3D anomaly detection model, Memory-to-Prototype Anomaly Detection (M2P-AD), which effectively models the distribution of normal features while suppressing excessive anomaly scores in normal regions and false positives near object boundaries. Specifically, we introduce a Memory-to-Prototype (M2P) module that learns representative prototypes from normal feature embeddings to preserve important structural information of objects. In addition, a Boundary extraction (BE) module is integrated to identify object boundaries, and a Boundary-aware score refinement (BSR) strategy is applied to recalibrate anomaly scores by incorporating boundary characteristics. The proposed method is evaluated on Real3D-AD, Anomaly-ShapeNet, and MulSen-AD, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Qualitative results demonstrate that excessive anomaly scores in normal regions are reduced and false positives near object boundaries are suppressed, resulting in more accurate and stable anomaly localization. The results indicate that the proposed approach enables more reliable 3D anomaly detection and provides a robust solution applicable to real-world industrial environments.

中文摘要翻译: 3D异常检测最近已成为计算机视觉中的一个重要研究课题。尽管现有方法已取得高性能，但正常区域中过高的异常响应以及物体边界附近的误报仍然是未解决的挑战。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种新颖的3D异常检测模型——记忆到原型异常检测（M2P-AD），该模型能够有效建模正常特征分布，同时抑制正常区域中的过高异常分数和物体边界附近的误报。具体而言，我们引入了一个记忆到原型（M2P）模块，从正常特征嵌入中学习代表性原型，以保留物体的重要结构信息。此外，集成了边界提取（BE）模块来识别物体边界，并应用边界感知评分修正（BSR）策略，通过结合边界特征重新校准异常分数。所提出的方法在Real3D-AD、Anomaly-ShapeNet和MulSen-AD上进行了评估，取得了最先进的性能。定性结果表明，正常区域中的过高异常分数被降低，物体边界附近的误报被抑制，从而实现更准确和稳定的异常定位。这些结果表明，所提出的方法能够实现更可靠的3D异常检测，并为实际工业环境提供了一种鲁棒的解决方案。

</details>


### [38] [Attention-Free and Lightweight Token Reduction for Efficient Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13500)
*Xuanyi Hao,Zuoyuan Zhang,Zhibo Wang,Xiaoyi Pang,Jiahui Hu,Jiacheng Du,Shuguo Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种即插即用的无注意力轻量级token精简框架，通过基于熵的信息度量和变换一致性信号，在保持关键与多样性token的同时，显著加速VLM推理，实现精度-效率的优越权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉token精简方法要么依赖与现代加速框架不兼容的注意力图，要么依赖计算密集的成对相似度比较，导致可扩展性差，难以在资源受限的边缘设备上高效部署VLM。

Method: 1）无注意力重要性估计：从信息论视角出发，采用基于熵的新准则量化token信息量，保留表达力更强、退化程度更低的token。2）轻量级多样性覆盖：引入变换一致性信号，相似token产生相似信号，通过排序和跨步选择获得多样性token集。

Result: 在多个VLM基准上的大量实验表明，该框架在激进压缩下仍能保持有竞争力的性能，实现了良好的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的无注意力轻量级token精简框架作为即插即用模块，能有效兼顾重要性与多样性，在显著降低计算开销的同时保持模型性能，适合边缘设备部署。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance in multimodal understanding, yet remain challenging to deploy on resource-constrained edge devices due to the substantial computational overhead of processing numerous visual tokens. Token reduction is a promising direction for accelerating VLMs inference, but existing approaches either rely on attention maps that are incompatible with modern acceleration frameworks or depend on computationally intensive pairwise similarity comparisons, which undermine scalability and negate their practical benefits in deployment. In this paper, we propose an attention-free and lightweight token reduction framework as a plug-and-play module for VLMs, which preserves both important and diverse tokens to produce a compact visual representation. First, to enable attention-free importance estimation, we adopt an information-theoretic perspective and quantify token information using a novel entropy-based criterion, retaining those with more expressive and less degenerate feature representations. Second, to ensure diverse visual coverage in a lightweight manner, we introduce a transformation-induced consistency signal where similar tokens yield similar signals, such that sorting by this signal places similar tokens close to each other and enables stride-based selection to produce a diverse token set. Extensive experiments across multiple VLMs benchmarks demonstrate that our framework achieves a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off, maintaining competitive performance under aggressive compression.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在多模态理解任务中取得了优异性能，但由于处理大量视觉token需要巨大的计算开销，在资源受限的边缘设备上部署仍然具有挑战性。Token精简是加速VLM推理的一个有前景的方向，但现有方法要么依赖与现代加速框架不兼容的注意力图，要么依赖于计算密集的成对相似度比较，这不仅损害了可扩展性，也抵消了其在实际部署中的实用价值。本文提出了一种无注意力的轻量级token精简框架，作为VLM的即插即用模块，通过同时保留重要且多样的token来生成紧凑的视觉表示。首先，为了实现无注意力重要性估计，我们采用信息论视角，利用一种基于熵的新准则量化token信息量，保留那些具有更强表达力和更低退化程度的特征表示。其次，为了以轻量级方式确保视觉覆盖的多样性，我们引入一种变换一致性信号——相似的token产生相似的信号，从而通过对该信号进行排序使得相似token彼此靠近，进而通过跨步选择得到多样化的token集。在多个VLM基准上的大量实验表明，我们的框架实现了良好的精度-效率权衡，在激进压缩下仍能保持有竞争力的性能。

</details>


### [39] [DP-BOA: Dirichlet-Process Birth-or-Assign for On-the-Fly Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13504)
*Peiyan Gu,Zixin Teng,Xuming He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DP-BOA，一种基于在线狄利克雷过程高斯混合模型的后验预测决策框架，将新类别生成作为显式统计选择而非隐式回退，在OCD基准上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有在线类别发现方法通常基于匹配启发式规则（如半径或哈希）隐式地处理新类别生成，将其作为无匹配时的回退方案，缺乏自身的统计证据支持。本文旨在将新类别的生成建模为具有统计证据支持的显式决策选项。

Method: DP-BOA基于在线狄利克雷过程高斯混合模型（DP-GMM）和正态逆威沙特（NIW）先验。训练时用带标签数据校准共享NIW先验并初始化已知类别后验；测试时对每个样本比较"分配到已知类别"与"生成新类别"的后验预测证据，并在线更新类别统计量。

Result: 在标准OCD基准上，DP-BOA一致性地优于强基线方法，尤其在新型类别发现性能上表现突出，同时保持了有竞争力的已知类别准确率。

Conclusion: 将新类别生成建模为显式统计决策比隐式回退更有效；该方法能捕获各向异性的类别几何结构，并随证据积累自然调整决策置信度。

Original Abstract: On-the-fly category discovery requires deciding for each incoming test sample whether to assign it to an existing category or spawn a new one. Existing methods typically implement this decision through matching-based heuristics, such as radius- or hash-based rules. While effective in practice, these methods usually treat category birth implicitly as a fallback when no existing category matches confidently, rather than as an explicit alternative supported by its own statistical evidence. To address this, we propose DP-BOA, a posterior-predictive decision framework based on an online Dirichlet-process Gaussian mixture model with a Normal-Inverse-Wishart prior. During training, we use labeled data to calibrate a shared NIW prior over category Gaussians and warm-start the known-category posteriors. At test time, for each incoming sample, DP-BOA compares the posterior predictive evidence for assignment to existing categories against the evidence for spawning a new category induced by the DP prior, and then updates category statistics online after the decision. The method captures anisotropic category geometry and naturally adapts decision confidence as evidence accumulates. Across standard OCD benchmarks, DP-BOA consistently outperforms strong baselines and delivers particularly strong novel-class discovery performance while maintaining competitive known-class accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 在线类别发现需要决定每个到来的测试样本是分配给一个已有类别还是生成一个新类别。现有方法通常通过基于匹配的启发式规则（如半径或哈希规则）来实现这一决策。虽然这些方法在实践中有效，但它们通常将类别生成隐式地视为当没有类别能够可靠匹配时的回退方案，而非作为具有自身统计证据支持的显式替代选项。为解决这一问题，我们提出DP-BOA，一种基于在线狄利克雷过程高斯混合模型（配备正态逆威沙特先验）的后验预测决策框架。在训练阶段，我们使用带标签数据校准一个共享的NIW先验（用于类别高斯分布）并热启动已知类别的后验分布。在测试阶段，对于每个输入样本，DP-BOA比较将其分配到已知类别的后验预测证据与由DP先验诱导的生成新类别的证据，并在决策后在线更新类别统计量。该方法能够捕获各向异性的类别几何结构，并随着证据的积累自然调整决策置信度。在标准OCD基准上，DP-BOA一致性地优于强基线方法，在新型类别发现任务上表现尤为突出，同时保持了有竞争力的已知类别准确率。

</details>


### [40] [TRACE-PCa: Predicting Prostate Cancer Progression from Longitudinal MRI During Active Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13506)
*Hongye Zeng,Shreeram Athreya,Dingyuan Dai,Steve Raman,Leonard Marks,William Speier,Corey Arnold*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需病灶分割的端到端时序多模态模型，用于预测前列腺癌主动监测期间的病理进展。该模型使用预训练3D MRI基础模型编码序列影像，通过时序注意力门控机制放大与进展相关的影像变化，并与临床变量融合进行预测。在纵向队列中性能优于基线，与放射科医生评估相当，具有减少不必要活检的潜力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前主动监测（AS）策略依赖定期重复活检，但大多数活检未发现进展且不必要。现有风险分层工具仅基于单时间点影像或依赖病灶分割，无法捕捉纵向变化，且排除了无可视病灶的患者。

Method: 提出端到端时序多模态模型：(1) 使用预训练3D MRI基础模型编码每次序列扫描；(2) 引入时序注意力门控机制，重新校准多次随访的影像特征，放大与进展相关的局灶性影像变化；(3) 将门控影像表示与临床变量在多模态框架中融合，估计进展概率。

Result: 在纵向AS队列中，模型持续优于竞争基线方法，性能与代表当前临床实践的放射科医生评估相当。在保持高阴性预测值的同时实现了更高的阳性预测值。

Conclusion: 该模型有潜力在监测期间安全地减少不必要的活检。

Original Abstract: Active surveillance (AS) is the preferred strategy for favorable-risk prostate cancer, yet current protocols rely on scheduled repeat biopsies, most of which reveal no progression and are unnecessary. Existing risk-stratification tools operate on single time-point imaging or depend on explicit lesion segmentation, limiting their ability to capture longitudinal change and excluding patients without an MRI-visible lesion. In this study, we propose an end-to-end temporal and multimodal model for predicting pathological progression during AS without lesion segmentation. We encode each serial scan with a pretrained 3D MRI foundation model and introduce a temporal attention gate that recalibrates the multi-visit features to amplify focal imaging changes associated with progression. The gated imaging representation is then fused with clinical variables in a multimodal framework to estimate the probability of progression. Validated on a longitudinal AS cohort, our approach consistently outperforms competing baselines and performs comparably to the radiologist assessment representing current clinical practice. It maintains high negative predictive value while achieving higher positive predictive value, demonstrating its potential to safely reduce unnecessary biopsies during surveillance.

中文摘要翻译: 主动监测（Active Surveillance, AS）是低风险前列腺癌的首选策略，然而当前方案依赖定期重复活检，而大多数活检并未显示进展且非必要。现有的风险分层工具基于单时间点影像或依赖明确的病灶分割，限制了其捕获纵向变化的能力，并且排除了无可视MRI病灶的患者。在本研究中，我们提出了一种无需病灶分割的端到端时序多模态模型，用于预测主动监测期间的病理进展。我们使用预训练的3D MRI基础模型对每次序列扫描进行编码，并引入一个时序注意力门控机制，重新校准多次随访的影像特征，以放大与进展相关的局灶性影像变化。随后，将门控影像表示与临床变量在多模态框架中进行融合，以估计进展概率。在一个纵向主动监测队列上验证，我们的方法持续优于竞争基线方法，且性能与代表当前临床实践的放射科医生评估相当。该方法在保持高阴性预测值的同时实现了更高的阳性预测值，证明了其在监测期间安全减少不必要活检的潜力。

</details>


### [41] [DriveFace: A Cross-Spectral Through-Glass Face Dataset for On-the-Move Vehicular Border Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13515)
*Anjith George,Luis Luevano,Alain Komaty,Zeina Al Amine,Vidit Vidit,Sebastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对边境管控场景下移动中人脸识别的数据缺失问题，构建了一个包含NIR车辆通行视频与智能手机预注册数据的真实场景数据集，实验表明现有SOTA模型性能显著下降，亟需专用方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有面部识别基准数据集均在受控环境中采集，无法捕捉车辆通行场景中的运动模糊、光照变化、遮挡及跨光谱注册等实际挑战，严重制约了移动中人脸识别技术的发展。

Method: 构建了一个面向边境管控场景的移动中人脸识别数据集，包含NIR（近红外）车辆通行视频和智能手机预注册数据，并使用当前最先进模型进行基线评估。

Result: 在最先进模型上进行基线评估的结果显示，在这些真实条件下模型的性能存在明显局限，表明现有方法无法有效应对车辆场景中的实际挑战。

Conclusion: 现有面部识别模型在边境管控的真实车辆通行场景中表现不佳，亟需开发专门针对移动中采集条件的方法来推动该领域的发展。

Original Abstract: The continuous growth in cross-border mobility places increasing pressure on existing border control infrastructures, motivating on-the-move biometric authentication, in which travellers are identified directly inside their vehicles at checkpoints. Face recognition is well-suited to this setting, as it can be acquired passively and at a distance. Its development, however, is hindered by the lack of representative datasets: existing benchmarks are collected in controlled environments and do not capture the challenges inherent to vehicular acquisition, including motion blur, variable illumination, occlusions, and cross-spectral enrollment. To address this gap, we introduce a dataset for on-the-move face recognition in border-control scenarios, comprising NIR vehicle-crossing videos paired with smartphone-based pre-enrollment data. Baseline evaluations with state-of-the-art models show clear performance limitations under these realistic conditions, highlighting the need for dedicated methods to advance the field.

中文摘要翻译: 跨境流动人口的持续增长给现有边境管控基础设施带来了日益沉重的压力，这推动了移动中生物特征认证技术的发展，即旅客在检查站直接在车内完成身份识别。人脸识别因其能够以被动方式远距离采集，非常适合这一场景。然而，缺乏具有代表性的数据集阻碍了其发展：现有基准数据集均在受控环境中采集，无法捕获车辆通行场景中固有的挑战，包括运动模糊、可变光照、遮挡以及跨光谱注册。为填补这一空白，我们引入了一个面向边境管控场景的移动中人脸识别数据集，包含NIR车辆通行视频与基于智能手机的预注册数据。使用当前最先进模型进行的基线评估显示，在这些真实条件下模型的性能存在明显局限，凸显了推动该领域发展需要专用方法。

</details>


### [42] [VGIF-Score: Interpretable and Diagnostic Evaluation of Spatio-Temporal Instruction Following in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13527)
*Songyu Xu,Xin Wang,Qiang Chen,Xinran Wang,Muxi Diao,Yuxuan Zhang,Kongming Liang,Rui Lin,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VGIF-Score 框架，通过 ST-DAG 解析和 AutoRubric 评估，对视频生成模型的指令跟随能力进行可解释、自动化评估，并在含 223 条长复杂指令的基准上验证了其可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成模型评估协议多依赖短语义浅的提示词，缺乏对长指令、组合约束和时空依赖的评估，且常依赖昂贵人工评估或手工视觉流水线，诊断能力不足。

Method: 提出 VGIF-Score，包含两个互补分支：(1) 客观完成分支：将提示解析为时空有向无环图 (ST-DAG)，执行依赖感知的 QA 与短路诊断；(2) 主观满意度分支：使用指令条件化的 AutoRubric 评估摄影、视觉纯净度、运动平滑度和物理合理性，最终融合为主观+客观统一评分。

Result: 在 14 个闭源和开源 VGM 上，对超过 3000 个生成视频进行实验，VGIF-Score 提供了可靠、可解释且具有诊断价值的指令跟随评估。

Conclusion: VGIF-Score 是一种高度自动化、可解释的评估框架，能有效诊断视频生成中指令约束的成败，为视频生成模型的指令跟随能力提供了系统化评估手段。

Original Abstract: Recent video generation models (VGMs) have made substantial progress in visual fidelity, yet their ability to follow long, compositional instructions remains insufficiently evaluated. Existing evaluation protocols often rely on prompts that are short and semantically shallow, with limited atomic constraints and weak spatio-temporal dependencies. They also frequently depend on costly human evaluation or handcrafted vision pipelines, while providing little diagnostic insight into which instruction constraints succeed or fail. To address this gap, we propose VGIF-Score, a highly automated and interpretable framework for evaluating instruction following in video generation. VGIF-Score consists of two complementary components: an objective completion branch that parses prompts into a Spatio-Temporal Directed Acyclic Graph (ST-DAG) and performs dependency-aware QA with short-circuit diagnostics, and a subjective satisfaction branch that uses instruction-conditioned AutoRubric to assess cinematography, visual purity, motion smoothness, and physics adherence. Together, these components produce a unified score that captures both objective completion and perceptual satisfaction. We instantiate this framework on VGIF-Bench, a benchmark of 223 long, structurally entangled prompts paired with approximately 4.3K fine-grained evaluation items. Experiments on 14 proprietary and open-source VGMs across more than 3K generated videos show that VGIF-Score provides reliable, interpretable, and diagnostically useful evaluation of video generation instruction following. The code will be available at https://github.com/PRIS-CV/VGIF-SCORE.

中文摘要翻译: 近期视频生成模型（VGMs）在视觉保真度方面取得了显著进展，但它们遵循长序列、组合性指令的能力仍未得到充分评估。现有评估协议通常依赖简短且语义浅显的提示词，原子约束有限且时空依赖性较弱。这些方法还常常依赖昂贵的人工评估或手工设计的视觉流水线，同时对哪些指令约束成功或失败几乎无法提供诊断性信息。为弥补这一空缺，我们提出 VGIF-Score，一个高度自动化且可解释的视频生成指令跟随评估框架。VGIF-Score 包含两个互补分支：客观完成分支将提示解析为时空有向无环图（ST-DAG），并执行依赖感知的问答与短路诊断；主观满意度分支则使用指令条件化的 AutoRubric 评估摄影手法、视觉纯净度、运动平滑性和物理合理性。两者共同产生一个统一评分，同时捕捉客观完成度与感知满意度。我们基于 VGIF-Bench 基准（包含 223 条长序列、结构纠缠的提示，配以约 4300 个细粒度评估项）实例化该框架。在 14 个闭源和开源 VGM 上进行的实验（涵盖超过 3000 个生成视频）表明，VGIF-Score 为视频生成的指令跟随提供了可靠、可解释且具有诊断价值的评估。代码将发布于 https://github.com/PRIS-CV/VGIF-SCORE。

</details>


### [43] [ThinkBLOX: 3D Indoor Scene Generation with Progressive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13539)
*Yuan Xiao,Can Wang,Xiangyu Kong,Jing Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ThinkBLOX提出了一种基于VLM的渐进式推理框架，通过逐步推理与动作生成3D室内场景，并配套构建了ThinkBLOX-Data-200K数据集和Tier-Decoupled GDPO强化学习方案，在物理合理性、语义对齐和交互编辑性上显著优于当前主流的一步式与迭代式基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统自回归或层次化方法合成3D室内场景效率较低，而近期基于VLM的一步式生成范式虽然高效，但在交互编辑（如插入或移动物体）时需要进行全局重优化或完全重建，且容易产生物理或语义上组织混乱的布局。为此，作者希望设计一种支持逐步推理与增量更新的渐进式生成框架，以提升场景编辑的灵活性和布局质量。

Method: (1) 提出ThinkBLOX框架，将3D布局生成建模为状态条件化的逐步推理与动作过程；(2) 构建ThinkBLOX-Data-200K数据集，包含224,757个程序化放置对，并标注了多视角场景上下文、显式思维链（CoT）推理过程和结构化JSON布局；(3) 通过监督微调（SFT）使VLM学会在增量更新下弥合推理与动作之间的差距；(4) 提出Tier-Decoupled GDPO强化学习方案，将异质奖励组织为不同层级，分别针对物理有效性、语义合理性和推理-动作一致性进行稳定策略优化。

Result: 实验表明，ThinkBLOX在物理合理性、语义对齐和交互编辑性上显著优于近期的一步式生成和迭代式基线方法。同时，该方法支持多种应用，包括全局生成、局部生成以及3D场景的重排。

Conclusion: ThinkBLOX通过渐进式推理与增量更新的方式，有效克服了一步式VLM生成方法在交互编辑中的局限性，为3D室内场景的灵活生成与编辑提供了一种新范式。Tier-Decoupled GDPO方案也为多目标优化下的策略学习提供了有效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: While traditional graphics methods often synthesize 3D indoor scenes autoregressively or hierarchically, recent vision-language model (VLM)-based generators predominantly adopt a one-shot paradigm where the full layout is planned at once. This one-shot approach often requires global re-optimization or complete reconstruction during interactive editing (e.g., inserting or moving objects) and can lead to physically or semantically poorly organized arrangements. To address these challenges, we propose ThinkBLOX, a VLM-based progressive reasoning framework that iteratively designs and refines 3D scenes. ThinkBLOX treats layout generation as a state-conditioned, step-by-step reasoningand-action process. To power this, we construct the ThinkBLOX-Data-200K dataset, containing 224,757 procedural placement pairs annotated with multi-view scene context, explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) rationales, and structured JSON layouts. Through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on this dataset, the VLM learns to bridge the reasoning-action gap under incremental updates. Furthermore, recognizing that scene synthesis is inherently a multisolution task where SFT suffers from reward conflict, we introduce Tier-Decoupled GDPO. This reinforcement learning scheme organizes heterogeneous rewards into distinct tiers, stabilizing policy optimization across physical validity, semantic plausibility, and reasoning-action consistency. Extensive experiments show that ThinkBLOX significantly outperforms recent one-shot and iterative baselines in physical plausibility, semantic alignment, and interactive editability. Additionally, we show that it supports diverse applications, including both global and local generation and rearrangement of 3D scenes.

中文摘要翻译: 传统图形方法通常以自回归或层次化方式合成3D室内场景，而近期基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）的生成器主要采用一步式范式，一次性规划完整布局。这种一步式方法在交互式编辑（例如插入或移动物体）时通常需要全局重优化或完全重建，并可能导致物理或语义上组织混乱的布局。为应对这些挑战，我们提出ThinkBLOX，一个基于VLM的渐进式推理框架，能够迭代式地设计和优化3D场景。ThinkBLOX将布局生成视为一个状态条件化的逐步推理与动作过程。为此，我们构建了ThinkBLOX-Data-200K数据集，包含224,757个程序化放置对，并标注了多视角场景上下文、显式思维链（CoT）推理过程以及结构化JSON布局。通过在该数据集上进行监督微调（SFT），VLM学会了在增量更新下弥合推理与动作之间的差距。此外，鉴于场景合成本质上是一个多解任务，且SFT面临奖励冲突问题，我们引入了层级解耦的GDPO（Tier-Decoupled GDPO）。该强化学习方案将异质奖励组织为不同层级，在物理有效性、语义合理性以及推理-动作一致性上稳定策略优化。大量实验表明，ThinkBLOX在物理合理性、语义对齐和交互编辑性上显著优于近期的一步式和迭代式基线方法。此外，我们还展示了ThinkBLOX支持多种应用，包括3D场景的全局与局部生成以及重排。

</details>


### [44] [Nexus: Native Mesh Generation with Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13563)
*Hanxiao Wang,Ying-Tian Liu,Yuan-Chen Guo,Qi-Yuan Feng,Zi-Xin Zou,Ding Liang,Biao Zhang,Yan-Pei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Nexus，一种通过解耦顶点生成和拓扑生成的扩散方法，实现高质量整体网格生成。顶点采用基于八叉树的稀疏体素扩散模型从粗到细生成，拓扑采用时空间隔（Spacetime Interval）编码边和面拓扑为连续顶点嵌入，再用扩散模型生成嵌入。实验在Objaverse和Toys4K数据集及真实图像上优于自回归和两阶段基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 主流网格生成方法依赖网格序列化和自回归过程，存在推理效率低、误差累积敏感等问题。为解决这些固有局限，需要一种能够整体生成高质量三角网格的新方法。

Method: 1) 将网格顶点视为八叉树组织的稀疏体素，采用扩散模型以从粗到细的方式生成顶点；2) 提出时空间隔（Spacetime Interval），作为时空距离（Spacetime Distance）的扩展，将任意边和面拓扑编码为连续的逐顶点嵌入，实现全局高效的复杂拓扑恢复；3) 在生成的顶点上使用扩散模型生成连续嵌入，从而恢复完整拓扑。

Result: 在Objaverse和Toys4K数据集以及真实图像上的大量实验表明，该方法优于最先进的自回归和两阶段基线方法。盲人用户研究（来自3D从业者）确认了感知偏好。

Conclusion: Nexus通过解耦顶点和拓扑生成的扩散方法，有效规避了顺序网格建模的固有限制，实现了高质量的整体网格生成。

Original Abstract: Generating high-quality triangle meshes is essential for film, gaming, and interactive 3D applications. Mainstream methods rely on mesh serialization and autoregressive processes, which stuggles in effective inference and is sensitive to error accumulation. In this paper, we present Nexus, a diffusion method that achieves holistic mesh generation via decoupled vertex and topology generation. First, we view mesh vertices as sparse voxels organized as an octree and adopt a diffusion model to generate the vertices in a coarse-to-fine manner. Second, for topology modeling, we propose Spacetime Interval, as an extension of Spacetime Distance to encode arbitrary edge and face topology into continuous per-vertex embeddings. It allows for a global and efficient recovery of complex topology. We then employ a diffusion model to generate the continuous embeddings on the generated vertices. Extensive experiments on the Objaverse and Toys4K datasets and in-the-wild images demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art autoregressive and two-stage baselines, effectively circumventing the inherent limitations of sequential mesh modeling. A blind user study from 3D practitioners confirms strong perceptual preference for our results.

中文摘要翻译: 生成高质量的三角网格对于电影、游戏和交互式3D应用至关重要。主流方法依赖网格序列化和自回归过程，这在有效推理方面存在困难，且对误差累积敏感。在本文中，我们提出了Nexus，一种通过解耦顶点生成和拓扑生成来实现整体网格生成的扩散方法。首先，我们将网格顶点视为以八叉树组织的稀疏体素，并采用扩散模型以从粗到细的方式生成顶点。其次，针对拓扑建模，我们提出了时空间隔（Spacetime Interval），作为时空距离（Spacetime Distance）的扩展，将任意边和面拓扑编码为连续的逐顶点嵌入，从而能够全局且高效地恢复复杂拓扑。然后，我们使用扩散模型在生成的顶点上生成连续嵌入。在Objaverse和Toys4K数据集以及真实图像上的大量实验表明，我们的方法优于最先进的自回归和两阶段基线方法，有效规避了顺序网格建模的固有限制。一项来自3D从业者的盲人用户研究证实了用户对我们结果的强烈感知偏好。

</details>


### [45] [GHR-VLM: Making Zero-Shot Transit Video Analytics Realizable with Grounded Hybrid Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13569)
*Kaicong Huang,Weiheng Oh,Ruimin Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GHR-VLM，一种视觉基础混合推理框架，通过边缘-云协同设计（轻量级边缘监控+后端VLM）实现零样本公交视频支付行为分析，在486分钟真实视频上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 监督视频模型需要任务特定标注，而直接将视觉语言模型（VLM）应用于长时车载视频既不可靠且成本高昂，因此需要结合两者优势的混合方法。

Method: 提出边缘-云协同设计：边缘端轻量级监控器持续追踪车门状态并分割乘客片段，后端VLM通过两阶段由粗到细的时空证据精炼来识别上车乘客并分类支付行为，仅对有基础证据的乘客片段和联系单调用VLM。

Result: 在486分钟真实公交监控视频上验证了基于基础证据的边缘-云推理在乘客级支付分析中的潜力，同时揭示了退化视频条件带来的挑战。

Conclusion: 显式视觉基础能够通过将长时监控流转换为紧凑的乘客中心时空证据来提升VLM推理能力，GHR-VLM减少了云端推理开销，避免了支付特定训练数据，并提供了VLM难以自行定位的局部证据。

Original Abstract: Transit video understanding can provide valuable fine-grained data that conventional passenger counters and fare systems cannot capture. However, supervised video models require task-specific annotations, while applying vision-language models (VLMs) directly to long onboard videos is unreliable and costly. To leverage the complementary strengths of both approaches, we propose GHR-VLM, a visual grounded hybrid reasoning framework for zero-shot transit-bus video analytics. It is motivated by the observation that explicit visual grounding can improve VLM reasoning by converting long surveillance streams into compact, passenger-centered spatiotemporal evidence. Specifically, we propose an edge-cloud design in which a lightweight edge-based monitor continuously tracks door status and segments passenger clips. A backend VLM then identifies boarding passengers and classifies payment behavior through a two-stage coarse-to-fine refinement of spatiotemporal evidence. By invoking the VLM only on grounded passenger clips and contact sheets, GHR-VLM reduces cloud inference, avoids payment-specific training data, and supplies the localized evidence that VLMs otherwise struggle to identify. Evaluation on 486 minutes of real-world bus surveillance video demonstrates the potential of grounded edge-cloud reasoning for passenger-level payment analytics while highlighting the challenges posed by degraded video conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 公交视频理解能够提供传统乘客计数器和票务系统无法捕获的细粒度数据。然而，监督视频模型需要任务特定的标注，而直接将视觉语言模型（VLM）应用于长时车载视频既不可靠且成本高昂。为结合两种方法的互补优势，我们提出GHR-VLM，一种用于零样本公交视频分析的视觉基础混合推理框架。其动机源于以下观察：显式视觉基础能够通过将长时监控流转换为紧凑的、以乘客为中心的时空证据来提升VLM推理能力。具体而言，我们提出一种边缘-云协同设计，其中轻量级边缘端监控器持续追踪车门状态并分割乘客片段，后端VLM通过两阶段由粗到细的时空证据精炼来识别上车乘客并分类支付行为。通过仅对有基础的乘客片段和联系单调用VLM，GHR-VLM减少了云端推理开销，避免了支付特定训练数据，并提供了VLM难以自行定位的局部证据。在486分钟真实公交监控视频上的评估表明，基于基础证据的边缘-云推理在乘客级支付分析中具有潜力，同时也揭示了退化视频条件带来的挑战。

</details>


### [46] [UniPhysGen: Unified Physical Grounding for Simulation-Ready 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13586)
*Xian Li,Rong Wei,Lujie Yang,Haolin Huang,Junyuan Fang,Siliang Tang,Jun Xiao,Rui Tang,Juncheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniPhys框架，可将原始3D资产自动转换为具有统一物理语义（关节语义和内在物理属性）的仿真就绪资产，并构建了大规模数据集UniPhys-40K和评测基准UniPhys-Bench，以及统一物理接地模型UniPhysGen，在关节接地和物理属性估计任务上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D资产缺乏统一的物理语义（关节语义和内在物理属性），无法直接用于具身AI和机器人仿真中的真实交互；现有方法要么独立处理这些语义，要么依赖规范化对象结构，对异构3D资产的鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出UniPhys可扩展框架自动转换原始3D资产；构建UniPhys-40K数据集和UniPhys-Bench基准；提出UniPhysGen统一物理接地模型，联合推理关节语义和内在物理属性，并引入几何鲁棒的关节接地机制以缓解异构部件分解下的几何捷径偏差。

Result: 在关节接地和内在物理属性估计任务上达到最先进性能；生成的资产可直接部署于机器人仿真环境进行真实物理交互。

Conclusion: UniPhys系列工作有效解决了3D资产统一物理语义标注的难题，为具身AI和机器人仿真提供了大规模物理接地数据集和高效标注框架。

Original Abstract: Physically grounded 3D assets are increasingly important for embodied AI and robotic simulation. However, most existing 3D assets lack unified physical semantics, including articulation semantics and intrinsic physical properties, required for realistic interaction. Current approaches either treat these semantics independently or rely on canonicalized object structures, limiting robustness across heterogeneous 3D assets. We present UniPhys, a scalable framework for automatically transforming raw 3D assets into simulation-ready assets with unified physical semantics. Based on UniPhys, we construct UniPhys-40K, a large-scale physically grounded dataset, together with UniPhys-Bench, a carefully verified benchmark for unified physical grounding evaluation. We further introduce UniPhysGen, a unified physical grounding model that jointly reasons over articulation semantics and intrinsic physical properties. UniPhysGen incorporates geometry-robust articulation grounding to mitigate geometric shortcut bias under heterogeneous part decompositions. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across articulation grounding and intrinsic physical property estimation tasks, while the resulting assets can be directly deployed in robotic simulation environments for realistic physical interaction. Our code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/breezexian/UniPhysGen.

中文摘要翻译: 基于物理的3D资产对于具身AI和机器人仿真日益重要。然而，大多数现有的3D资产缺乏用于真实交互所需的统一物理语义，包括关节语义和内在物理属性。当前方法要么独立处理这些语义，要么依赖规范化对象结构，限制了在异构3D资产上的鲁棒性。我们提出UniPhys，一个可扩展的框架，用于自动将原始3D资产转换为具有统一物理语义的仿真就绪资产。基于UniPhys，我们构建了UniPhys-40K，一个大规模基于物理的数据集，以及UniPhys-Bench，一个精心验证的统一物理接地评估基准。我们进一步提出了UniPhysGen，一个统一物理接地模型，联合推理关节语义和内在物理属性。UniPhysGen引入了几何鲁棒的关节接地机制，以缓解异构部件分解下的几何捷径偏差。大量实验表明，该方法在关节接地和内在物理属性估计任务上达到了最先进性能，生成的资产可直接部署于机器人仿真环境以进行真实的物理交互。我们的代码和数据集将在https://github.com/breezexian/UniPhysGen上公开。

</details>


### [47] [OvisOCR2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13639)
*Shiyin Lu,Yinglun Li,Yu Xia,Yuhui Chen,An-Yang Ji,Jun-Peng Jiang,Qing-Guo Chen,Jianshan Zhao,En Lin,Haijun Li,Cheng Qin,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OvisOCR2是一个0.8B参数的高效文档解析模型，采用端到端方式将文档页面图像转换为Markdown格式，通过数据引擎、强化学习、知识蒸馏和模型融合等技术，在多个基准上取得了最先进的结果，证明了端到端文档解析的潜力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的文档解析方法通常采用流水线（pipeline）方式，将检测、识别、布局分析等多个模块串联，流程复杂且错误累积。作者旨在探索端到端文档解析的潜力，设计一个简洁高效的模型，在性能上超越流水线方法。

Method: 1) 数据引擎：结合滤波后的真实文档标注与合成页面（渲染图像和Markdown目标来自同一HTML源）。2) 训练流程：监督微调 → 在4B分支上进行强化学习（多组件奖励设计）→ 在线蒸馏到0.8B模型 → 模型融合。

Result: 在OmniDocBench v1.6上以96.58的整体得分取得最优，首次让端到端模型登顶该榜单；在PureDocBench上取得75.06的Avg3最高分；在自建长尾困难基准上同样取得最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: OvisOCR2以0.8B的小参数量，通过端到端架构在多项文档解析基准上超越流水线方法，验证了端到端文档解析的巨大潜力，具备良好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: We introduce OvisOCR2, a 0.8B document parsing model. OvisOCR2 is designed as an end-to-end parser: given a document page image, it generates a Markdown representation in natural reading order, covering text, formulas, tables, and visual regions. We build a data engine that combines filtered real-document annotations with synthetic pages whose rendered images and Markdown targets are derived from the same HTML source. The training recipe includes supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning on a 4B branch with a multi-component reward design, on-policy distillation into the 0.8B model, and model fusion. On OmniDocBench v1.6, OvisOCR2 achieves a state-of-the-art overall score of 96.58, placing an end-to-end model at the top of this leaderboard previously dominated by pipeline methods and highlighting the potential of end-to-end document parsing. On PureDocBench, OvisOCR2 also achieves the highest Avg3 score of 75.06. Beyond these two public benchmarks, we evaluate OvisOCR2 on an in-house benchmark designed to cover a broader set of long-tail and challenging scenarios. OvisOCR2 obtains the best overall performance among the compared methods, providing further evidence of its generalization and robustness. OvisOCR2 is available at https://huggingface.co/ATH-MaaS/OvisOCR2.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了OvisOCR2，一个0.8B参数的文档解析模型。OvisOCR2被设计为端到端解析器：给定一张文档页面图像，它能够按照自然阅读顺序生成Markdown格式的表示，涵盖文本、公式、表格以及视觉区域。我们构建了一个数据引擎，结合了经过滤波的真实文档标注和合成页面，这些合成页面的渲染图像和Markdown目标均来源于相同的HTML源。训练流程包括监督微调、在4B分支上进行的强化学习（采用多组件奖励设计）、向0.8B模型的在线蒸馏以及模型融合。在OmniDocBench v1.6上，OvisOCR2取得了96.58的整体得分，达到最先进水平，使得端到端模型首次位居此前由流水线方法主导的榜单榜首，彰显了端到端文档解析的潜力。在PureDocBench上，OvisOCR2也取得了最高的Avg3分数75.06。除了这两个公开基准外，我们还在一个内部基准上评估了OvisOCR2，该基准旨在覆盖更广泛的长尾和具有挑战性的场景。OvisOCR2在对比方法中取得了最佳整体性能，进一步证明了其泛化能力和鲁棒性。OvisOCR2的模型已发布于https://huggingface.co/ATH-MaaS/OvisOCR2。

</details>


### [48] [Human4K: A Large-Scale 4K Multi-View Mocap Dataset for Whole-Body 3D Human Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13646)
*Tianshun Han,Ziyu Shi,Lijian Liu,Ajian Liu,Benjia Zhou,Hugo Jair Escalante,Yanyan Liang,Sergio Escalera,Zhen Lei,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Human4K——一个大规模4K多视角全身人体重建数据集，包含600万+张由八视图高速相机系统与Vicon动作捕捉系统同步采集的高分辨率图像，并配备高精度SMPL-X标注，有效解决了现有数据集在复杂真实场景下重建不稳定、肢体关节不准确等问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D人体重建方法在最具挑战性的真实场景中仍表现不佳，产生不稳定几何、不准确的肢体关节以及深度模糊或自遮挡下的不可靠预测。根本原因是现有数据集缺乏高分辨率图像、高精度标注和多样化全身运动的组合。

Method: Human4K数据集：使用八视图高分辨率相机系统与专业Vicon动作捕捉系统同步采集，涵盖11个受试者执行复杂、高关节运动且强自遮挡的全身动作，共600万+张4K图像；所有序列经运动重定向与细化模块（MRRM）处理以确保全身与四肢的精确对齐。

Result: 使用Human4K训练后，在标准基准上全身重建性能持续提升，尤其在手部、足部和深度模糊的肢体构型上取得显著改进。

Conclusion: Human4K数据集有效弥补了现有数据集的不足，通过高分辨率、高精度标注和多样化全身运动数据，显著提升了复杂场景下全身人体重建的鲁棒性和准确性。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in 3D human reconstruction have improved overall performance, yet current models still fail in the most challenging real-world scenarios. They often produce unstable geometry, inaccurate limb articulation and unreliable predictions under depth ambiguity or self-occlusion. A key reason is that existing datasets still lack the combination of high-resolution images, high-precision annotations and diverse whole-body motions required to support robust reconstruction. To address this gap, we present Human4K, a large-scale 4K multi-view whole-body human reconstruction dataset with mocap-accurate SMPL-X annotations. Human4K contains over six million 4K images captured by an eight-view high-resolution camera system synchronized with a professional Vicon motion capture setup, covering 11 subjects performing complex, highly articulated and strongly self-occluded full-body motions. All sequences are processed by a Motion-Retargeting and Refinement Module (MRRM) to ensure precise alignment for the full body and extremities. Experimental results show that training with Human4K consistently improves whole-body reconstruction on standard benchmarks, with particularly large gains for hands, feet and depth-ambiguous limb configurations.

中文摘要翻译: 近期3D人体重建的进展提升了整体性能，但现有模型在最具挑战性的真实场景中仍然失败。它们通常产生不稳定的几何结构、不准确的肢体关节以及在深度模糊或自遮挡下的不可靠预测。其关键原因在于现有数据集仍缺乏支持鲁棒重建所需的高分辨率图像、高精度标注和多样化全身运动的组合。为解决这一差距，我们提出了Human4K——一个大规模4K多视角全身人体重建数据集，配备动作捕捉级精度的SMPL-X标注。Human4K包含由八视图高分辨率相机系统与专业Vicon动作捕捉系统同步采集的超过六百万张4K图像，覆盖11名受试者执行复杂、高关节运动且强自遮挡的全身动作。所有序列均经过运动重定向与细化模块（MRRM）处理，以确保全身与四肢的精确对齐。实验结果表明，使用Human4K训练能够在标准基准上持续改善全身重建效果，其中在手部、足部和深度模糊的肢体构型上提升尤为显著。

</details>


### [49] [Beyond Color Geometry: Evaluating Human-Like Color Representations in Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13647)
*Ayan Igali,Pakizar Shamoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模糊感知模型框架，用于评估视觉模型是否像人类一样组织颜色。该框架从类别边界、类别紧凑性和分级对齐三个维度评估颜色表征，并发现不同Vision Transformer编码器在分级对齐上差异显著，其中Masked Autoencoder表现最佳。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有颜色表征评估通常依赖CIELAB等几何空间或离散颜色标签，这些方法能捕捉感知距离或类别成员关系，但无法反映人类组织颜色的分级（graded）方式。因此需要一种能评估模型与人类颜色感知分级对齐程度的方法。

Method: 构建一个包含86个分级类别的模糊感知模型，该模型基于人类调查数据拟合而成。该框架可应用于任意图像编码器，测量三个互补性质：类别边界（category boundaries）、类别紧凑性（category compactness）以及超越颜色几何结构所能解释的分级对齐（graded alignment）。

Result: 对11个Vision Transformer编码器的评估显示：类别层面的结果大致相似，但分级对齐差异显著。Masked Autoencoder（MAE）在超越几何对齐上表现最强，置信区间与其他编码器不重叠。逐层分析表明掩码重建从输入到输出保持了该结构。在自然图像上，MAE全局表征表面颜色，而语言监督模型（如CLIP）则更强烈地编码与前景物体相关的颜色。

Conclusion: 类人颜色感知具有多个不同的方面，不应简化为单一分数。MAE在颜色感知上与人类对齐最好，不同训练范式（掩码重建 vs. 语言监督）导致颜色表征在自然图像中存在根本性差异。

Original Abstract: Do vision models see colors the way humans do? Existing evaluations of color representations usually compare them with geometric spaces such as CIELAB or with discrete color labels. These references capture perceptual distance or category membership, but not the graded way in which people organize colors. We evaluate color grounding against a fuzzy perceptual model with 86 graded categories fitted to human survey data. The framework can be applied to any image encoder and measures three complementary properties: category boundaries, category compactness, and graded alignment beyond what color geometry alone can explain. Across eleven Vision Transformer encoders, the category-level results are broadly similar, whereas graded alignment differs substantially. Masked Autoencoders achieve the strongest beyond-geometry alignment, with confidence intervals that do not overlap those of the other encoders. A layer-wise analysis further shows that masked reconstruction preserves this structure toward the output. On natural images, MAE represents surface color globally, while language-supervised models encode color more strongly in relation to the foreground object. These results show that human-like color grounding has several distinct aspects that should not be reduced to a single score.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉模型是否像人类一样感知颜色？现有的颜色表征评估通常将其与CIELAB等几何空间或离散颜色标签进行比较。这些参照能捕捉感知距离或类别成员关系，但无法反映人类组织颜色的分级方式。本文针对一个包含86个分级类别的模糊感知模型评估颜色对齐，该模型基于人类调查数据拟合而成。该框架可应用于任意图像编码器，并测量三个互补性质：类别边界、类别紧凑性，以及超越颜色几何结构所能解释的分级对齐。对11个Vision Transformer编码器的评估显示，类别层面的结果大致相似，而分级对齐则差异显著。Masked Autoencoder在超越几何对齐上表现最强，其置信区间与其他编码器不重叠。逐层分析进一步表明，掩码重建在向输出层传播时保留了这一结构。在自然图像上，MAE全局地表征表面颜色，而语言监督模型则更强烈地编码与前景物体相关的颜色。这些结果表明，类人颜色感知包含多个不同的方面，不应被简化为单一分数。

</details>


### [50] [From Surface Forecasting to Observability Forecasting: A Latent World Model for Cloud-Aware EO Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13651)
*Mohanad Albughdadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LeWorldModel，一种基于联合嵌入预测架构的世界模型，用于地球观测中的可观测性预测。该模型利用多光谱影像和气象驱动数据，预测下一景影像是否可用以及何时可恢复可用视图。在EarthNet2021数据集上，模型在多项基准测试中优于持久性模型和LightGBM基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 地球观测处理链的瓶颈不在于新影像的获取，而在于影像到达时地表是否实际可见（即是否存在云覆盖）。因此需要研究可观测性预测问题，提前判断影像是否可用以及何时能获得可用视图。

Method: 采用LeWorldModel这一联合嵌入预测架构的世界模型，适配云感知的地球观测序列。将原始minicube转换为HDF5序列，包含5个影像通道（蓝、绿、红、近红外、云掩膜）和8个气象与历法协变量。模型共18.0M可训练参数，在23,904个训练片段上从头训练。评估采用锁定协议：线性探针仅在训练集拟合，验证集上设置校准，然后冻结头部进行valsplit、IID、OOD和极端评估。

Result: 在冻结可观测性基准上，LeWorldModel持续优于持久性模型。下一步可用性预测的平衡准确率为0.769-0.887（持久性为0.493-0.556）；首次可用视界预测准确率为0.602-0.806（持久性为0.120-0.369）。与LightGBM对比，LeWM在连续晴/云回归和精确恢复时序上更优，而LightGBM在简单二分类任务和OOD鲁棒性上更强。此外，LeWM在合成时间不一致性下能产生强排序异常信号。

Conclusion: LeWorldModel能有效预测地球观测影像的可观测性，在多项任务上优于持久性基线，并与LightGBM各有优势。模型还具备检测时间异常的能力，为地球观测数据质量控制提供了新工具。

Original Abstract: The bottleneck of Earth Observation processing chains is not the arrival of new imagery but whether the surface is actually visible when the image arrives. We study this as an observability forecasting problem on EarthNet2021. Given recent multispectral imagery and exogenous weather drivers, the goal is to predict whether the next acquisition will be usable and, if not, when a usable view is likely to return. To do this, we adapt LeWorldModel, a joint-embedding predictive architecture world model, to cloud-aware Earth Observation sequences. The final pipeline converts raw minicubes into episodic HDF5 sequences with five image channels (blue, green, red, near-infrared, cloud mask) and eight meteorological and calendar covariates. The resulting model has 18.0M trainable parameters and is trained from scratch on 23,904 training episodes. The trained leWorldModel is evaluated under a locked protocol: linear probes are fit on train only, calibration choices are set on an internal validation split, and the fitted heads are then frozen for valsplit, IID, OOD, and extreme evaluation. On the full frozen-bundle observability benchmark, LeWorldModel consistently outperforms persistence. For next-step usability, balanced accuracy ranges from 0.769 to 0.887, compared with 0.493 to 0.556 for persistence. For exact first-usable-horizon prediction, accuracy ranges from 0.602 to 0.806, compared with 0.120 to 0.369 for persistence. Against a frozen LightGBM baseline fit on the same training windows, LeWorldModel is better on continuous clear/cloud regression and on exact recovery timing on valsplit, IID, and extreme, while LightGBM is stronger on the simpler binary any-usable-within-six task and is more robust on OOD. In separate sampled diagnostic analyses, LeWM also produces strong ranking-based anomaly signals under synthetic temporal inconsistencies.

中文摘要翻译: 地球观测处理链的瓶颈并非新影像的到达，而是影像到达时地表是否实际可见。我们将其作为一个可观测性预测问题，在EarthNet2021数据集上进行研究。给定近期多光谱影像和外源气象驱动数据，目标是预测下一次获取的影像是否可用，以及如果不可用，何时可能恢复可用视图。为此，我们改进了LeWorldModel——一种联合嵌入预测架构的世界模型——使其适应云感知的地球观测序列。最终流程将原始minicube转换为情节性HDF5序列，包含五个影像通道（蓝、绿、红、近红外、云掩膜）和八个气象与历法协变量。该模型共有18.0M可训练参数，在23,904个训练情节上从头训练。训练后的LeWorldModel在锁定协议下进行评估：线性探针仅在训练集上拟合，校准选择在内部验证集上设定，然后冻结训练好的分类/回归头部，用于验证集、独立同分布、分布外和极端评估。在完整的冻结束可观测性基准上，LeWorldModel持续优于持久性模型。对于下一步可用性预测，平衡准确率范围为0.769至0.887，而持久性模型为0.493至0.556。对于精确首次可用视界预测，准确率范围为0.602至0.806，而持久性模型为0.120至0.369。与基于相同训练窗口拟合的冻结LightGBM基线相比，LeWorldModel在连续晴/云回归和精确恢复时序方面在验证集、独立同分布和极端场景下表现更好，而LightGBM在更简单的六步内任意可用二分类任务上更强，且在分布外场景下更鲁棒。在分离的样本诊断分析中，LeWM在合成时间不一致性下也产生了强排序的异常信号。

</details>


### [51] [Exploratory, Communicative, and Deployable: Vision-Driven Embodied Agents for Open-World Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13653)
*Boyu Mi,Mengchen Ma,Yifei Yao,Xing Gao,Junting Chen,Yangzi Li,Zihou Zhu,Guohao Li,Zhenfei Yin,Tai Wang,Yao Mu,Jiangmiao Pang,Hanqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出REAL框架，用于开放世界移动操作任务，通过模拟到现实一致的API设计、模拟用户交互、分层训练范式（数据收集+监督微调+在线强化学习）以及REAL-Bench基准，在物理双机械臂移动机器人上实现了78.3%的端到端成功率，展现了强大的零样本迁移能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有具身智能框架在部署时往往依赖特权模拟器状态或假设指令完全明确，忽视了主动探索、视觉定位和交互式意图消歧等现实部署挑战，导致模拟与现实之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出了REAL框架，包含：(1) 无需先验感知的模拟-现实一致环境API；(2) 集成模拟用户实现人在回路交互；(3) 多样化任务组合驱动数据收集、监督微调和在线强化学习的分层训练流程；(4) REAL-Bench基准（241个任务，涵盖主动探索、视觉干扰、关节物体操作和交互消歧）。

Result: (1) 在交互任务上以56.9%成功率超越商用闭源VLM；(2) 分层训练有效对齐模型工具使用能力并保持开放词汇推理；(3) 在物理双机械臂移动机器人上60个真实场景中达到78.3%端到端成功率，实现零样本迁移到未见家庭场景。

Conclusion: REAL框架通过模拟-现实一致的设计成功弥合了长时程移动操作任务中的虚实差距，验证了从仿真到真实世界的零样本可迁移性。

Original Abstract: Real-world deployment of embodied agents requires active exploration, visual grounding, and interactive intent disambiguation. However, existing frameworks often rely on privileged simulator states or assume complete instructions, bypassing realistic deployment challenges. To bridge this gap, we present REAL, an agentic framework for open-world mobile manipulation. REAL establishes sim-to-real-consistent environment APIs without oracle perception and integrates a simulated user to enable human-in-the-loop interaction. Within this environment, we design diverse task compositions to drive data collection, supervised fine-tuning, and online reinforcement learning, systematically optimizing agent performance. To comprehensively evaluate this approach, we introduce REAL-Bench, a benchmark spanning 241 tasks across active exploration, visual distraction, articulated manipulation, and interactive disambiguation.
  Experimental results demonstrate that our trained agent outperforms leading commercial closed-source VLMs on interactive tasks with a 56.9% success rate. Further empirical analysis reveals that our hierarchical training pipeline successfully aligns the model's tool-use capabilities while maintaining robust open-vocabulary reasoning under extended exploration horizons. Finally, we deploy and evaluate our framework on a physical dual-arm mobile robot, where it achieves a 78.3% end-to-end success rate over 60 real-world episodes. These physical trials demonstrate robust zero-shot transferability to unseen household scenarios, validating that our sim-to-real-consistent design successfully bridges the reality gap for long-horizon mobile manipulation. Code is available at https://github.com/InternRobotics/REAL.

中文摘要翻译: 具身智能体在现实世界的部署需要主动探索、视觉定位和交互式意图消歧能力。然而，现有框架通常依赖特权的模拟器状态或假设指令完全明确，绕过了现实部署中的挑战。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了REAL——一个面向开放世界移动操作的自主体框架。REAL构建了无需先验感知的、模拟-现实一致的环境API，并集成模拟用户以实现人在回路交互。在该环境中，我们设计了多样化的任务组合以驱动数据收集、监督微调和在线强化学习，系统性地优化智能体性能。为全面评估这一方法，我们引入了REAL-Bench，一个涵盖241个任务的基准，涉及主动探索、视觉干扰、关节物体操作和交互式消歧。实验结果表明，我们训练的智能体在交互任务上以56.9%的成功率超越了领先的商业闭源视觉语言模型。进一步的实证分析揭示，我们的分层训练流程成功对齐了模型的工具使用能力，同时在扩展探索视野下保持了鲁棒的开放词汇推理。最后，我们在物理双机械臂移动机器人上部署并评估了我们的框架，在60个真实世界场景中实现了78.3%的端到端成功率。这些物理试验展示了在未见家庭场景中的鲁棒零样本可迁移性，验证了我们的模拟-现实一致设计成功弥合了长时程移动操作任务中的虚实鸿沟。代码已开源：https://github.com/InternRobotics/REAL。

</details>


### [52] [T3HG-Editor: Text-driven 3D Human Garment Editing with Body Priors Embedded in SMPL-X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13654)
*Shaoru Sun,Xingtao Wang,Zihan Ma,Wenrui Li,Jiantao Zhou,Debin Zhao,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出T3HG-Editor，一种基于SMPL-X几何与关节先验的文本驱动3D人体服装编辑方法，通过三阶段流程（可编辑高斯获取、服装一致性编辑、带溢出修剪的高斯更新）实现高保真且服装一致的编辑效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D高斯编辑方法扩展到人体服装编辑时存在保真度低、存在伪影和服装不一致的问题；SMPL-X模型蕴含丰富的虚拟人体先验信息，为突破这一瓶颈提供了机会。

Method: 三阶段流程：(1) 可编辑高斯获取：沿SMPL-X法线播种高斯生成近表面高斯，再用2D掩码约束定位目标高斯；(2) 服装一致性编辑：聚合多视角下对应同一SMPL-X顶点的token并传播回原视角，无需额外训练；(3) 高斯更新与溢出修剪：基于SMPL-X构建SDF距离场，结合2D语义掩码修剪溢出高斯，防止污染非目标区域。

Result: 在多个受试者和多种服装类型上的实验表明，T3HG-Editor在编辑质量和服装一致性方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 基于SMPL-X先验的T3HG-Editor能有效解决3D人体服装编辑中的保真度和一致性问题，为文本驱动的3D虚拟试衣和人像编辑提供了高质量解决方案。

Original Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Editing (3DGE) has seen substantial progress, text-driven 3D human garment editing remains largely underexplored. Existing 3DGE works typically follow a paradigm that applies 2D editing techniques to multi-view rendered images and updates 3D Gaussians based on the modified images. Extending such methods to 3D human garment editing suffers from low-fidelity outcomes, caused by introduced distortions and garment inconsistencies. A promising breakthrough opportunity arises from the SMPL eXpressive (SMPL-X) model that embodies rich prior information for virtual humans. Motivated by this insight, we propose a text-driven 3D human garment editor termed T3HG-Editor, which delivers high-fidelity and garment consistent results by leveraging geometry and joint priors embedded in SMPL-X. Specifically, T3HG-Editor contains three stages, namely obtainment of editable Gaussians, garment consistent editing, and Gaussian updating with overflow pruning. The obtainment of editable Gaussians begins with seeding Gaussians along SMPL-X normals to generate sufficient near surface Gaussians, followed by a 2D mask constraint that precisely localizes the target Gaussians to be edited. The garment consistent editing aggregates tokens corresponding to the same SMPL-X vertex across multiple views and propagates them to their original views, enforcing garment consistency without requiring additional training. Gaussian updating with overflow pruning employs a Signed Distance Function (SDF) defined on SMPL-X to construct a human distance field, which is then integrated with a 2D semantic mask to prune overflowing Gaussians, thus preventing contamination of non-target regions. Experiments on multiple subjects and diverse garment types demonstrate that T3HG-Editor outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both editing quality and garment consistency.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管3D高斯编辑（3DGE）已取得显著进展，但文本驱动的3D人体服装编辑仍鲜有探索。现有3DGE工作通常遵循如下范式：将2D编辑技术应用于多视角渲染图像，然后基于修改后的图像更新3D高斯。将此类方法扩展到3D人体服装编辑时会因引入的畸变和服装不一致而导致低保真结果。SMPL表达性（SMPL-X）模型蕴含丰富的虚拟人体先验信息，为突破这一瓶颈提供了契机。受此启发，我们提出一种名为T3HG-Editor的文本驱动3D人体服装编辑器，通过利用SMPL-X中嵌入的几何和关节先验，实现高保真且服装一致的结果。具体而言，T3HG-Editor包含三个阶段：可编辑高斯的获取、服装一致性编辑以及带溢出修剪的高斯更新。可编辑高斯的获取首先沿SMPL-X法线播种高斯以生成充足的近表面高斯，然后通过2D掩码约束精确定位待编辑的目标高斯。服装一致性编辑聚合多视角下对应同一SMPL-X顶点的token，并将其传播回原始视角，从而在不需额外训练的情况下强制执行服装一致性。带溢出修剪的高斯更新利用SMPL-X上定义的符号距离函数（SDF）构建人体距离场，再与2D语义掩码结合，修剪溢出的高斯，从而防止非目标区域被污染。在多个受试者和多种服装类型上的实验表明，T3HG-Editor在编辑质量和服装一致性方面均优于现有最先进方法。

</details>


### [53] [FreeLit: Paired-Free Indoor Relighting via Physics-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13656)
*Chi-En Yen,Duy-Khanh Ngo,Wen-Wei Tang,Huu-Phu Do,Wen-Hsiao Peng,Ching-Chun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeLit提出了一种无需配对数据的室内场景重打光框架，通过物理引导的照明先验和内在属性稳定策略，实现了对光源位置、颜色和强度的显式控制，尤其在低光环境下具有鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的数据驱动方法依赖昂贵的配对多照明数据集，难以扩展；逆向渲染方法虽减少数据依赖，但在复杂条件下内在估计鲁棒性不足。因此，需要一种无需配对数据、能显式控制光源属性且鲁棒的重打光方法。

Method: 提出FreeLit框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 从内在场景属性构建物理引导的照明先验，生成结构化光图与伪重打光图像以引导扩散模型合成；(2) 重打光引导的内在稳定化策略，通过结构感知蒸馏和一致性约束强制反射率在光照下不变；(3) 面向可控性的评估指标，量化与用户指定光照颜色和强度的对齐程度。

Result: 实验表明FreeLit在无需配对监督的条件下，实现了稳定、物理一致且可控的重打光，在低光室内场景中具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FreeLit证明通过物理先验引导和内在属性稳定策略，可以在无需昂贵配对数据的情况下实现可控且鲁棒的室内场景重打光。

Original Abstract: Image-based indoor scene relighting remains challenging due to the complex interplay between cluttered geometry and local illumination, requiring precise modeling of light position, color, and intensity. Existing data-driven methods implicitly learn this relationship via paired multi-illumination datasets. Nevertheless, this data is costly and fails to scale, which is essential for accurate light-source-level control. Conversely, inverse-rendering methods reduce the data dependency by incorporating physical priors; however, they lack the robustness of intrinsic estimation in challenging conditions.
  In this paper, we present FreeLit, a paired-free framework for controllable indoor relighting that explicitly manipulates light-source location, color, and intensity. Instead of relying on paired supervision, we construct a physics-guided illumination prior from intrinsic scene properties, generating a structured lightmap along with a pseudo-relit image to guide diffusion-based synthesis. To address instability in intrinsic estimation, especially in low-light scenes, we introduce a relighting-guided intrinsic stabilization strategy that enforces illumination-invariant reflectance through structure-aware distillation and consistency constraints. Furthermore, we propose controllability-oriented evaluation metrics to quantify alignment with user-specified illumination color and intensity. Experimental results demonstrate that FreeLit achieves stable, physically consistent, and controllable relighting, with improved robustness in low-light indoor scenes, without requiring paired supervision.

中文摘要翻译: 基于图像的重打光由于杂乱几何与局部光照之间复杂的相互作用而仍然具有挑战性，需要精确建模光源位置、颜色和强度。现有数据驱动方法通过配对的多照明数据集隐式学习这种关系，然而这类数据成本高昂且难以扩展，而这对于精确的光源级控制至关重要。相反，逆向渲染方法通过引入物理先验减少了数据依赖性，但在具有挑战性的条件下缺乏内在估计的鲁棒性。本文提出FreeLit，一种无需配对的可控室内重打光框架，能够显式地操控光源位置、颜色和强度。我们不依赖配对监督，而是从内在场景属性构建物理引导的照明先验，生成结构化光图以及伪重打光图像来引导基于扩散模型的合成。为了解决内在估计的不稳定性（特别是在低光场景中），我们提出了一种重打光引导的内在稳定化策略，通过结构感知蒸馏和一致性约束来强制反射率在光照下保持不变。此外，我们提出了面向可控性的评估指标，用于量化与用户指定的光照颜色和强度的对齐程度。实验结果表明，FreeLit实现了稳定、物理一致且可控的重打光，在低光室内场景中具有更好的鲁棒性，且无需配对监督。

</details>


### [54] [Fine-grained CLIP fine-tuning with self-annotated region alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13661)
*Chenyang Zhao,Wei Lin,Antoni B. Chan,Janet H. Hsiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SFF-CLIP，一种仅使用图像-文本对进行微调的方法，在不依赖额外区域标注且不牺牲全局表示能力的前提下，提升CLIP的细粒度密集特征表示能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有CLIP微调方法存在两个主要局限：(1)通常需要额外区域标注，受限于预定义类别且数据处理成本高；(2)往往会牺牲CLIP原有的全局视觉表示能力。因此需要一种无需额外标注、同时保持全局语义一致性的细粒度微调方法。

Method: 提出运行时区域-短语对齐方案：从输入句子中提取概念短语，利用文本特异性热力图将其与对应的基于区域的视觉特征进行对齐，仅使用图像-文本对作为输入进行微调。

Result: 实验表明SFF-CLIP在细粒度密集特征表示任务上取得显著性能提升，同时在图像级任务上保持了原始CLIP的性能。

Conclusion: SFF-CLIP通过在微调过程中仅使用图像-文本对，有效提升了CLIP的细粒度表示能力，同时不牺牲其全局视觉-语义一致性，为CLIP微调提供了一种更高效且语义更丰富的方案。

Original Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) has been shown to have limitations in its fine-grained dense feature representation, due to its pre-training focusing on matching the whole image to a text description. Considering the large data and computational burden in pre-training a vision-language model from scratch, a series of works aim to enhance the fine-grained ability of CLIP through a fine-tuning scheme. However, existing works suffer from a variety of limitations: additional region annotations are usually required, which limits the semantic diversity due to the predefined categories and leads to a large effort to process the training data; and they usually sacrifice CLIP's original ability for global visual representation. To bypass these limitations, we propose SFF-CLIP (Self-annotated Fine-grained Fine-tuning for CLIP), which only uses image-text pairs as input to boost the fine-grained representation ability in the CLIP fine-tuning, while maintaining the global visual-semantic consistency. Concretely, a run-time region-phrase alignment scheme is designed, which obtains concept phrases from the input sentence, and aligns them with corresponding extracted region-based features using text-specific heat maps. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SFF-CLIP leads to significant performance improvements on fine-grained dense feature representation, as well as maintaining the performance of the original CLIP on image-level tasks. Code will be released later.

中文摘要翻译: 对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）已被证明其细粒度密集特征表示能力存在局限性，原因在于其预训练过程侧重于将整幅图像与文本描述进行匹配。考虑到从头预训练视觉-语言模型所需的大量数据和计算负担，一系列工作旨在通过微调方案来增强CLIP的细粒度能力。然而，现有方法存在多种局限：通常需要额外的区域标注，这不仅因预定义类别限制了语义多样性，还导致处理训练数据的大量工作；同时，它们往往牺牲了CLIP原有的全局视觉表示能力。为绕过这些局限，我们提出了SFF-CLIP（自标注细粒度微调方法），该方法仅使用图像-文本对作为输入，在CLIP微调中提升细粒度表示能力，同时保持全局视觉-语义一致性。具体而言，我们设计了一种运行时区域-短语对齐方案，从输入句子中提取概念短语，并利用文本特异性热力图将其与对应的基于区域的特征进行对齐。大量实验表明，SFF-CLIP在细粒度密集特征表示上取得了显著的性能提升，同时在图像级任务上保持了原始CLIP的性能。代码将在后续发布。

</details>


### [55] [Learning Speaker Identity Beyond Language and Modality Constraints: Insights from the POLY-SIM 2026 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13669)
*Marta Moscati,Muhammad Saad Saeed,Marina Zanoni,Mubashir Noman,Rohan Kumar Das,Monorama Swain,Yassin Terraf,Yufang Hou,Elisabeth Andre,Khalid Mahmood Malik,Markus Schedl,Shah Nawaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文指出多模态说话人识别系统通常假设训练和测试时音视频模态完整且每个说话人只说一种语言，但这些假设在现实中常不成立。POLY-SIM 2026挑战赛旨在解决这些鲁棒性和泛化问题，并提供标准化比较框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实应用中多模态说话人识别面临两大挑战：一是视觉或音频信息可能因遮挡、设备故障或隐私限制而缺失；二是多语言说话人因语言变异性带来额外复杂性。现有系统缺乏应对这些情况的鲁棒性与泛化能力，因此亟需标准化评估平台推动该方向研究。

Method: 该文为挑战赛介绍，未提出具体算法方法，而是通过组织POLY-SIM 2026挑战赛，提供标准化的数据集、评估协议和比较基准，让参赛者提出各自的解决方案。

Result: 文中未呈现具体实验结果，主要目标是建立标准化评估框架，预期通过挑战赛比较不同方法的性能。

Conclusion: 多模态说话人识别系统需要在模态不完整和多语言场景下提升鲁棒性，POLY-SIM 2026挑战赛为相关研究提供了标准化比较平台。

Original Abstract: Multimodal speaker identification systems typically assume the availability of complete and homogeneous audio-visual modalities during both training and testing, and assume each speaker only speaks a single language. However, in real-world applications, such assumptions often do not hold. Visual or audio information may be missing due to occlusions, camera or microphone failures, or privacy constraints. Multilingual speakers introduce additional complexity due to linguistic variability across languages. These situations constitute substantial challenges for the robustness and generalization capabilities of multimodal speaker identification systems. Aim of the POLY-SIM 2026 challenge is to address these aspects of speaker identification and to provide a standardized setup for the comparison of the proposed solutions.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态说话人识别系统通常假设在训练和测试阶段都能获取完整且同质的音视频模态，并假设每个说话人只说一种语言。然而，在实际应用中，这些假设往往不成立。视觉或音频信息可能因遮挡、摄像头或麦克风故障，或隐私约束而缺失。多语言说话人由于语言间的变异性带来了额外的复杂性。这些情况对多模态说话人识别系统的鲁棒性和泛化能力构成了重大挑战。POLY-SIM 2026挑战赛旨在解决说话人识别在这些方面的难题，并为所提出的解决方案提供一个标准化的比较平台。

</details>


### [56] [Multimodal Assessment of Pancreatic Cancer Resectability Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13826)
*Vincent Ochs,Christoph Kuemmerli,Florentin Bieder,Julia Wolleb,Joel L. Lavanchy,Julia Ruppel,Jan Liechti,Stephanie Taha-Mehlitz,Christian Andreas Nebiker,Beat Mueller,Giuseppe Kito Fusai,Joerg-Matthias Pollok,Anas Taha,Philippe C. Cattin,Sebastian Staubli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个全自动多模态深度学习框架，联合分析3D增强CT和结构化临床信息，用于胰腺导管腺癌（PDAC）的可切除性三分类（可切除、交界可切除、局部晚期）。该框架采用Swin-UNETR骨干网络进行胰腺、肿瘤和血管的辅助分割，融合17项临床变量嵌入，并通过动态多任务目标平衡分割与分类任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 胰腺导管腺癌（PDAC）可切除性的准确判断依赖于CT影像中肿瘤与胰周主要血管的关系评估，但专家评估常存在显著的主观差异性，亟需一种自动化的客观评估方法。

Method: 采用Swin-UNETR作为骨干网络获取解剖感知的图像表示（通过胰腺、肿瘤和血管结构的辅助分割），将图像特征与17项常规临床变量的紧凑嵌入进行融合，再由轻量级分类头进行NCCN三分类输出。训练时采用动态多任务目标函数，根据当前肿瘤Dice性能自适应调整分割与分类之间的损失权重。

Result: 未在摘要中提供具体数值结果，仅描述了方法设计与训练策略。

Conclusion: 该方法通过多模态融合与动态多任务学习，能够生成既具有解剖信息又具有判别力的特征表示，有望实现PDAC可切除性的自动化客观评估。

Original Abstract: Accurate determination of pancreatic ductal adenocarcinoma (PDAC) resectability relies on evaluating how the tumor interacts with major peripancreatic vessels on CT imaging, yet expert assessment often shows substantial variability. We introduce a fully automated multimodal deep learning framework that jointly analyzes 3D contrast enhanced CT and structured clinical information to classify patients into the three National Comprehensive Cancer Network (NCCN) resectability categories (upfront resectable, borderline resectable, locally advanced). The approach uses a Swin-UNETR backbone to obtain anatomy aware image representations through auxiliary segmentation of pancreas, tumor, and vascular structures. These features are fused with a compact clinical embedding derived from 17 routinely collected variables and processed by a lightweight classification head. Model training is guided by a dynamic multitask objective that adapts the balance between segmentation and classification based on current tumor Dice performance, promoting feature representations that remain both anatomically informed and discriminative.

中文摘要翻译: 胰腺导管腺癌（PDAC）可切除性的准确判断依赖于CT影像中肿瘤与胰周主要血管相互作用的评估，但专家评估常表现出显著变异性。我们提出了一种全自动多模态深度学习框架，该框架联合分析三维增强CT和结构化临床信息，将患者分为美国国家综合癌症网络（NCCN）定义的三个可切除性类别（初始可切除、交界可切除、局部晚期）。该方法使用Swin-UNETR骨干网络，通过胰腺、肿瘤和血管结构的辅助分割获得解剖感知的图像表示。这些特征与从17项常规采集变量中提取的紧凑临床嵌入进行融合，并由轻量级分类头处理。模型训练由动态多任务目标引导，该目标根据当前肿瘤Dice性能自适应调整分割与分类之间的平衡，从而促进生成既具有解剖信息又具有判别力的特征表示。

</details>


### [57] [WAVE-Stereo: Warp-Aligned Volume Encoding for Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13674)
*Zehan Liu,Yage He,Xianwu Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAVE-Stereo提出了一种新的迭代立体匹配方法，通过GeoWarp对应编码器（GWCE）并行编码匹配搜索、残差对齐和视差先验，以及周期性全局上下文传播（PGCP）缓解无纹理区域的匹配退化，在多个真实基准上达到高精度与实时性的平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有迭代立体匹配方法将显式匹配搜索（相关体）和局部残差细化（扭曲特征）分别建模，未能利用二者互补的匹配线索，且无纹理区域匹配退化问题尚未有效解决。

Method: 提出WAVE-Stereo方法：1) GeoWarp对应编码器（GWCE）在ConvGRU输入阶段并行编码三种互补信息——相关体积的显式匹配搜索、扭曲特征的残差对齐以及视差先验；2) 周期性全局上下文传播（PGCP）以周期性方式传播全局空间信息，缓解无纹理区域的匹配退化。

Result: 在Middlebury、ETH3D、KITTI 2012/2015和Booster五个真实基准上，零样本泛化取得竞争性结果：KITTI 2015上3.18% D1-all，Booster上4.42% Bad-2.0，推理速度66ms，实现精度与效率的良好平衡。

Conclusion: WAVE-Stereo通过结合相关体积和特征扭曲的互补匹配线索，以及周期性全局上下文传播，在不依赖外部基础模型先验的情况下，实现了高精度的零样本泛化和实时推理，验证了方法的有效性。

Original Abstract: Existing iterative stereo matching methods primarily adopt two types of correspondence representation: explicit matching search via correlation volumes and local residual refinement via warped features, yet the two remain separately modeled. We propose WAVE-Stereo, built on a core insight: correlation volumes and feature warping provide complementary matching cues. \textbf{GeoWarp Correspondence Encoder (GWCE)} encodes matching search, residual alignment, and disparity prior in parallel at the ConvGRU input. To mitigate matching degradation in textureless regions, we propose \textbf{Periodic Global Context Propagation (PGCP)}, which propagates global spatial information in a periodic manner. On five real-world benchmarks -- Middlebury, ETH3D, KITTI 2012, KITTI 2015, and Booster -- WAVE-Stereo achieves competitive zero-shot generalization accuracy without any external foundation model prior, achieving 3.18\% D1-all on KITTI 2015, 4.42\% Bad-2.0 on Booster, and 66ms real-time inference, striking a favorable balance between accuracy and efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/yamanoko-do/WAVE-Stereo.

中文摘要翻译: 现有迭代立体匹配方法主要采用两种对应表示方式：通过相关体积进行显式匹配搜索，以及通过扭曲特征进行局部残差细化，然而这两种方式仍被分别建模。我们提出WAVE-Stereo，其核心洞见在于：相关体积和特征扭曲提供了互补的匹配线索。\textbf{GeoWarp对应编码器（GWCE）}在ConvGRU输入阶段并行编码匹配搜索、残差对齐和视差先验。为缓解无纹理区域的匹配退化，我们提出\textbf{周期性全局上下文传播（PGCP）}，以周期性方式传播全局空间信息。在Middlebury、ETH3D、KITTI 2012、KITTI 2015和Booster五个真实世界基准上，WAVE-Stereo在不依赖任何外部基础模型先验的情况下取得了具有竞争力的零样本泛化精度，在KITTI 2015上达到3.18%的D1-all，在Booster上达到4.42%的Bad-2.0，推理速度为66ms，实现了精度与效率的良好平衡。我们的代码已开源在https://github.com/yamanoko-do/WAVE-Stereo。

</details>


### [58] [The 2nd International StepUP Competition for Biometric Footstep Recognition: From Steps to Strides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13905)
*Robyn Larracy,Anant Gupta,Gourav Gupta,Ethan Eddy,Maxime Devanne,Cyril Meyer,Jin-Chern Chiou,Yueh-Shan Lee,Zong-Han Lu,Aaron Tabor,Erik Scheme*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了第二届International StepUP Competition（国际步态足迹生物特征识别竞赛）的框架、挑战、参赛方案及结果。竞赛基于大规模StepUP-P150数据集，聚焦三个关键挑战：有限注册数据下的泛化、鞋履与速度变化导致的域偏移、以及左右脚足迹的融合。最佳方案（ArogyaPandit Research Team）使用时空CNN结合集成评分策略达到8.00%的等错误率。结果表明，利用时序模式与推理时归一化/校准策略可提升性能，但在未见过的个人鞋履条件下识别用户仍是难点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 推动基于压力的足迹生物特征识别研究，通过标准化且具有挑战性的评估框架（即竞赛形式）来检验算法在极端跨域条件下的泛化能力、鲁棒性以及左右脚融合能力，从而促进该领域的前沿进展。

Method: 使用大规模StepUP-P150数据集（含超过20万条高分辨率动态足迹，来自150名个体）及未公开的测试集，设计三个挑战任务：未见用户且注册数据有限、鞋履与速度变化导致的域偏移、步幅级左右脚足迹融合。竞赛吸引26个注册团队，最佳方案为ArogyaPandit Research Team提出的时空卷积神经网络（Spatiotemporal CNN）结合集成评分策略（ensemble-based scoring strategy）。

Result: 最佳方案（ArogyaPandit Research Team）达到8.00%的等错误率（Equal Error Rate）。顶尖方案展示了利用时序模式、推理时归一化与校准策略对评分性能的提升效果。但识别未见过的个人鞋履条件下的用户仍具挑战性，尤其在存在相似特征干扰项时。

Conclusion: 基于压力的足迹生物特征识别在标准化竞赛框架下取得了显著进展，时空CNN与集成策略相结合的方法表现最佳。利用时序信息和推理时归一化/校准是提升性能的关键手段。然而，现实应用中个人鞋履变化带来的域偏移问题仍是制约该技术实用化的核心挑战。

Original Abstract: The International StepUP Competition Series was launched to advance research in pressure-based footstep biometrics through a standardized and challenging evaluation framework.
